"""SearchJev: training of a calibrated System-1 decision model on Qwen3.5 backbones (JEV row format).

The package is split into light-weight, torch-free modules (``labels``,
``schema``, ``prompting``, ``config``, ``metrics``) and heavier modules that
import torch/transformers/peft (``data``, ``collator``, ``modeling``, ``loss``,
``trainer``, ``calibration``, ``inference``).  Importing the package itself
never imports torch, so the light modules can be used in data-preparation
processes without CUDA initialisation.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

__version__ = "0.1.0"

__all__ = ["__version__"]



================================================
FILE: training/bootstrap.py
================================================
"""Interpreter bootstrap for the one-click scripts.

``start_training.py`` may be launched with the system ``python3`` (which has no
torch).  :func:`ensure_environment` detects that situation and re-executes the
current process with the project virtual environment, so that
``python3 start_training.py`` "just works" once the environment is installed.

Candidate interpreters, in order:

1. ``$JEV_PYTHON`` (explicit override)
2. ``<project>/.venv/bin/python``

Only the standard library is used here; the module must import before torch.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import importlib.util
import os
import sys
from pathlib import Path

PROJECT_ROOT: Path = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
REQUIRED_MODULES: tuple[str, ...] = ("torch", "transformers", "peft", "yaml", "numpy")
_GUARD_ENV = "JEV_BOOTSTRAPPED"


def missing_modules(modules: tuple[str, ...] = REQUIRED_MODULES) -> list[str]:
    """Names of the required modules that the *current* interpreter cannot import."""
    out: list[str] = []
    for name in modules:
        try:
            if importlib.util.find_spec(name) is None:
                out.append(name)
        except (ImportError, ValueError):  # broken namespace / stale finder
            out.append(name)
    return out


def candidate_interpreters() -> list[str]:
    """Interpreters to try, most specific first (duplicates and self removed)."""
    cands = [
        os.environ.get("JEV_PYTHON"),
        str(PROJECT_ROOT / ".venv" / "bin" / "python"),
    ]
    # NOTE: do not resolve symlinks - a venv's bin/python is usually a symlink to
    # the base interpreter, and it must be executed through the venv path to
    # pick up the venv's site-packages.
    current = os.path.abspath(sys.executable)
    seen: set[str] = set()
    out: list[str] = []
    for c in cands:
        if not c:
            continue
        path = os.path.abspath(os.path.expanduser(c))
        if path in seen or path == current:
            continue
        if os.path.isfile(path) and os.access(path, os.X_OK):
            seen.add(path)
            out.append(path)
    return out


def ensure_environment(argv: list[str] | None = None) -> None:
    """Re-exec with the project venv when the current interpreter lacks the ML stack.

    Does nothing when everything imports.  Prints a clear message and exits
    with code 3 when no usable interpreter is found.  A guard variable prevents
    exec loops.
    """
    missing = missing_modules()
    if not missing:
        return
    if os.environ.get(_GUARD_ENV) == "1":
        sys.stderr.write(
            f"[jev] interpreter {sys.executable} still lacks {missing} after bootstrap; "
            "install the requirements (see README.md) to (re)create the environment.\n"
        )
        sys.exit(3)
    for python in candidate_interpreters():
        os.environ[_GUARD_ENV] = "1"
        args = [python, *(argv if argv is not None else sys.argv)]
        sys.stderr.write(f"[jev] {sys.executable} lacks {missing}; re-executing with {python}\n")
        sys.stderr.flush()
        try:
            os.execv(python, args)
        except OSError as exc:  # pragma: no cover - try the next candidate
            sys.stderr.write(f"[jev] could not exec {python}: {exc}\n")
            continue
    sys.stderr.write(
        f"[jev] no Python environment with {missing} found. Install the requirements (see README.md) "
        "into ./.venv, or set JEV_PYTHON=/path/to/venv/bin/python.\n"
    )
    sys.exit(3)


__all__ = ["ensure_environment", "candidate_interpreters", "missing_modules"]



================================================
FILE: training/calibration.py
================================================
"""Post-hoc temperature scaling for JEV restricted-menu logits.

The JEV read-out produces a vector of raw logits ``z`` over the option menu
(canonical order, masked slots set to ``-inf``).  Temperature scaling replaces
``softmax(z)`` by ``softmax(z / T)`` where the scalar ``T > 0`` is fitted on a
held-out calibration split by minimising the negative log-likelihood of the
gold option.  ``T`` does not change the arg-max, only the confidence, so
accuracy is unchanged while NLL / Brier / ECE usually improve.

The fit is performed on ``log T`` with L-BFGS, optionally one temperature per
question type (``choice`` / ``noul`` / ``score``) on top of a global one.  The
result is a small JSON-serialisable dict written to ``<run>/calibration.json``.

All functions accept ``torch.Tensor`` or ``numpy.ndarray`` inputs and run on
CPU; no GPU or model weights are required.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import math
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Mapping

import numpy as np
import torch

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

#: Question-type ids used by the dataset / collator (mirrors ``training.data``).
QTYPE_TO_ID: dict[str, int] = {"choice": 0, "noul": 1, "score": 2}
ID_TO_QTYPE: dict[int, str] = {v: k for k, v in QTYPE_TO_ID.items()}
QTYPES: tuple[str, ...] = ("choice", "noul", "score")

#: Admissible temperature range (clamped after the fit).
T_MIN: float = 0.05
T_MAX: float = 50.0

#: Minimum number of examples required to fit a type-specific temperature;
#: below this the global temperature is reused for that type.
MIN_EXAMPLES_PER_TYPE: int = 10


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _as_float_tensor(x: Any) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Convert a tensor / array / nested list of logits to a float32 CPU tensor."""
    if isinstance(x, torch.Tensor):
        t = x.detach().to("cpu", dtype=torch.float32)
    else:
        t = torch.as_tensor(np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float32))
    if t.ndim == 1:
        t = t.unsqueeze(0)
    if t.ndim != 2:
        raise ValueError(f"logits must be [N, K]; got shape {tuple(t.shape)}")
    return t


def _as_long_tensor(x: Any) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Convert a tensor / array / list of integer ids to an int64 CPU tensor."""
    if isinstance(x, torch.Tensor):
        return x.detach().to("cpu", dtype=torch.long).reshape(-1)
    return torch.as_tensor(np.asarray(x).reshape(-1), dtype=torch.long)


def nll_at_temperature(logits: Any, target_idx: Any, temperature: float = 1.0) -> float:
    """Mean negative log-likelihood of the gold option at a given temperature.

    Args:
        logits: ``[N, K]`` raw menu logits, masked slots ``-inf``.
        target_idx: ``[N]`` gold option index (canonical order).
        temperature: scalar temperature ``T``; probabilities are ``softmax(z / T)``.

    Returns:
        Mean NLL in nats (``nan`` when ``N == 0``).
    """
    z = _as_float_tensor(logits)
    y = _as_long_tensor(target_idx)
    if z.shape[0] == 0:
        return float("nan")
    logp = torch.log_softmax(z / float(temperature), dim=-1)
    return float(-logp.gather(1, y.view(-1, 1)).mean())


def _valid_rows(z: torch.Tensor, y: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Boolean mask of rows whose gold slot is finite and within range."""
    n, k = z.shape
    in_range = (y >= 0) & (y < k)
    ok = torch.zeros(n, dtype=torch.bool)
    if in_range.any():
        gold = z[in_range].gather(1, y[in_range].view(-1, 1)).squeeze(1)
        ok[in_range] = torch.isfinite(gold)
    # every row also needs at least one finite logit (otherwise softmax is nan)
    ok &= torch.isfinite(z).any(dim=1)
    return ok


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# fitting
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def fit_temperature(
    logits: Any,
    target_idx: Any,
    init: float = 1.0,
    max_iter: int = 200,
) -> float:
    """Fit a single scalar temperature by minimising NLL with L-BFGS on ``log T``.

    Args:
        logits: ``[N, K]`` raw menu logits (canonical order, masked ``-inf``).
        target_idx: ``[N]`` gold indices.
        init: initial temperature.
        max_iter: maximum L-BFGS iterations.

    Returns:
        The fitted temperature, clamped to ``[T_MIN, T_MAX]``.  If the data are
        empty or the optimiser fails to improve on ``init``, ``init`` (clamped)
        is returned.
    """
    init = float(min(max(init, T_MIN), T_MAX))
    z = _as_float_tensor(logits)
    y = _as_long_tensor(target_idx)
    if z.shape[0] != y.shape[0]:
        raise ValueError(f"logits rows ({z.shape[0]}) != targets ({y.shape[0]})")
    keep = _valid_rows(z, y)
    n_bad = int((~keep).sum())
    if n_bad:
        logger.warning("fit_temperature: dropping %d rows with masked/invalid gold slot", n_bad)
        z, y = z[keep], y[keep]
    if z.shape[0] == 0:
        logger.warning("fit_temperature: no valid examples; returning T=%.3f", init)
        return init

    z = z.double()
    y = y.view(-1, 1)
    # masked (-inf) slots are zeroed before scaling and re-masked afterwards so
    # that no -inf enters the autograd graph (``-inf * exp(-log_t)`` -> nan grad)
    finite = torch.isfinite(z)
    z_finite = z.masked_fill(~finite, 0.0)
    log_t = torch.full((), math.log(init), dtype=torch.float64, requires_grad=True)
    lo, hi = math.log(T_MIN), math.log(T_MAX)

    def nll_of(lt: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
        scaled = (z_finite * torch.exp(-lt)).masked_fill(~finite, float("-inf"))
        logp = torch.log_softmax(scaled, dim=-1)
        return -logp.gather(1, y).mean()

    optimiser = torch.optim.LBFGS(
        [log_t],
        lr=1.0,
        max_iter=max_iter,
        tolerance_grad=1e-9,
        tolerance_change=1e-12,
        history_size=20,
        line_search_fn="strong_wolfe",
    )

    def closure() -> torch.Tensor:
        optimiser.zero_grad()
        loss = nll_of(log_t)
        loss.backward()
        return loss

    with torch.enable_grad():
        try:
            optimiser.step(closure)
        except RuntimeError as exc:  # pragma: no cover - numerical corner cases
            logger.warning("fit_temperature: L-BFGS failed (%s); keeping T=%.3f", exc, init)
            return init

    with torch.no_grad():
        lt = float(log_t.clamp(lo, hi))
        if not math.isfinite(lt):
            logger.warning("fit_temperature: non-finite result; keeping T=%.3f", init)
            return init
        fitted = float(nll_of(torch.tensor(lt, dtype=torch.float64)))
        baseline = float(nll_of(torch.tensor(math.log(init), dtype=torch.float64)))
    if not math.isfinite(fitted) or fitted > baseline + 1e-9:
        logger.warning(
            "fit_temperature: fitted T=%.4f did not improve NLL (%.5f vs %.5f); keeping T=%.3f",
            math.exp(lt), fitted, baseline, init,
        )
        return init
    t = math.exp(lt)
    logger.info("fit_temperature: n=%d  T=%.4f  nll %.5f -> %.5f", z.shape[0], t, baseline, fitted)
    return float(t)


def fit_calibration(
    logits: Any,
    target_idx: Any,
    qtype_id: Any,
    per_question_type: bool = True,
) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Fit global and (optionally) per-question-type temperatures.

    Args:
        logits: ``[N, K]`` raw menu logits, canonical order, masked ``-inf``.
        target_idx: ``[N]`` gold indices (canonical order).
        qtype_id: ``[N]`` question-type ids (``0=choice, 1=noul, 2=score``) or
            question-type names.
        per_question_type: fit a separate temperature per type.  Types with
            fewer than ``MIN_EXAMPLES_PER_TYPE`` examples reuse the global ``T``.

    Returns:
        ``{"global": T, "choice": T, "noul": T, "score": T,
        "n": {"total": N, "choice": n, ...}, "nll_before": .., "nll_after": ..,
        "per_type": {qtype: {"n", "nll_before", "nll_after"}},
        "per_question_type": bool, "t_min": .., "t_max": ..}``.
    """
    z = _as_float_tensor(logits)
    y = _as_long_tensor(target_idx)
    if isinstance(qtype_id, (list, tuple)) and qtype_id and isinstance(qtype_id[0], str):
        q = torch.tensor([QTYPE_TO_ID[str(s)] for s in qtype_id], dtype=torch.long)
    else:
        q = _as_long_tensor(qtype_id)
    n = int(z.shape[0])
    if not (y.shape[0] == n and q.shape[0] == n):
        raise ValueError("logits, target_idx and qtype_id must have the same length")

    t_global = fit_temperature(z, y) if n else 1.0
    out: dict[str, Any] = {
        "global": float(t_global),
        "n": {"total": n},
        "per_question_type": bool(per_question_type),
        "per_type": {},
        "t_min": T_MIN,
        "t_max": T_MAX,
    }

    # per-type temperatures
    for name in QTYPES:
        mask = q == QTYPE_TO_ID[name]
        n_t = int(mask.sum())
        out["n"][name] = n_t
        if per_question_type and n_t >= MIN_EXAMPLES_PER_TYPE:
            t_type = fit_temperature(z[mask], y[mask], init=t_global)
        else:
            if per_question_type and 0 < n_t < MIN_EXAMPLES_PER_TYPE:
                logger.info("calibration: only %d %s examples; using global T", n_t, name)
            t_type = t_global
        out[name] = float(t_type)
        if n_t:
            out["per_type"][name] = {
                "n": n_t,
                "temperature": float(t_type),
                "nll_before": nll_at_temperature(z[mask], y[mask], 1.0),
                "nll_after": nll_at_temperature(z[mask], y[mask], t_type),
            }

    # overall NLL before / after using the temperatures that will be applied
    if n:
        t_vec = torch.ones(n, dtype=torch.float32)
        for name in QTYPES:
            t_vec[q == QTYPE_TO_ID[name]] = float(out[name])
        keep = _valid_rows(z, y)
        zk, yk, tk = z[keep], y[keep].view(-1, 1), t_vec[keep].view(-1, 1)
        if zk.shape[0]:
            before = float(-torch.log_softmax(zk, -1).gather(1, yk).mean())
            after = float(-torch.log_softmax(zk / tk, -1).gather(1, yk).mean())
        else:
            before = after = float("nan")
    else:
        before = after = float("nan")
    out["nll_before"] = before
    out["nll_after"] = after
    logger.info(
        "calibration: n=%d global T=%.4f choice=%.4f noul=%.4f score=%.4f  nll %.5f -> %.5f",
        n, out["global"], out["choice"], out["noul"], out["score"], before, after,
    )
    return out


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# persistence / lookup
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _json_safe(obj: Any) -> Any:
    """Recursively convert numpy / torch scalars to plain Python types."""
    if isinstance(obj, Mapping):
        return {str(k): _json_safe(v) for k, v in obj.items()}
    if isinstance(obj, (list, tuple)):
        return [_json_safe(v) for v in obj]
    if isinstance(obj, torch.Tensor):
        return _json_safe(obj.tolist())
    if isinstance(obj, np.generic):
        return obj.item()
    if isinstance(obj, float) and not math.isfinite(obj):
        return None
    return obj


def save_calibration(d: Mapping[str, Any], path: str | Path) -> Path:
    """Write a calibration dict to ``path`` as JSON (parents created)."""
    p = Path(path)
    p.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with p.open("w", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        json.dump(_json_safe(d), fh, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
    logger.info("saved calibration to %s", p)
    return p


def load_calibration(path: str | Path) -> dict[str, Any] | None:
    """Load a calibration dict; returns ``None`` if the file is missing or invalid."""
    p = Path(path)
    if p.is_dir():
        p = p / "calibration.json"
    if not p.is_file():
        return None
    try:
        with p.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
            d = json.load(fh)
    except (OSError, json.JSONDecodeError) as exc:
        logger.warning("could not read calibration file %s: %s", p, exc)
        return None
    if not isinstance(d, dict):
        logger.warning("calibration file %s does not hold a dict; ignoring", p)
        return None
    return d


def _positive_float(v: Any) -> float | None:
    try:
        f = float(v)
    except (TypeError, ValueError):
        return None
    return f if math.isfinite(f) and f > 0 else None


def temperature_for(calib: Mapping[str, Any] | None, qtype: str) -> float:
    """Temperature to apply for ``qtype``: type-specific, else global, else ``1.0``."""
    if not calib:
        return 1.0
    for key in (qtype, "global"):
        t = _positive_float(calib.get(key))
        if t is not None:
            return t
    return 1.0


def apply_temperature(logits: Any, temperature: float) -> np.ndarray:
    """Return ``softmax(z / T)`` as a float64 numpy array (masked ``-inf`` -> 0)."""
    z = np.asarray(logits, dtype=np.float64) / float(temperature)
    z = z - np.max(z, axis=-1, keepdims=True)
    e = np.exp(z)
    return e / np.sum(e, axis=-1, keepdims=True)



================================================
FILE: training/collator.py
================================================
"""Batch collation for Qwen3.5-JEV.

:class:`JEVDataCollator` turns the per-example feature dicts produced by
:class:`training.data.JEVDataset` into padded tensors.  Prompts are
**left**-padded (the restricted read-out looks at the last position, so the
last column of ``attention_mask`` must be all ones) and the per-example
option "menus" are padded to the widest menu in the batch.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import inspect
import logging
from collections.abc import Mapping, Sequence
from typing import Any

import torch

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

__all__ = ["JEVDataCollator"]


class JEVDataCollator:
    """Collate JEV features into tensors.

    Output keys:

    * ``input_ids``, ``attention_mask`` -- ``[B, L]`` long, left-padded via
      ``tokenizer.pad`` (optionally to a multiple of ``pad_to_multiple_of``).
    * ``label_token_ids`` -- ``[B, Kmax]`` long, the vocabulary ids of the
      label tokens shown for each example (pad ``0``).
    * ``label_mask`` -- ``[B, Kmax]`` bool, ``True`` for real menu slots.
    * ``target_dist`` -- ``[B, Kmax]`` float32 target distribution in display
      order (pad ``0``).
    * ``target_idx`` -- ``[B]`` long display index of the gold option (``-1``
      when unlabeled).
    * ``qtype_id`` -- ``[B]`` long (choice=0, noul=1, score=2).
    * ``num_options`` -- ``[B]`` long.
    * ``perm`` -- ``[B, Kmax]`` long, display slot -> canonical index (pad ``-1``);
      lets the evaluation path map probabilities back to canonical order.
    """

    #: Default padding multiple.  128 is REQUIRED for training on Qwen3.5: the
    #: flash-linear-attention (tilelang) backward kernel used by the Gated-DeltaNet
    #: layers returns NaN gradients whenever the padded sequence length is an odd
    #: multiple of 64 (L % 128 == 64), verified on Qwen3.5-0.8B and -4B with fla 0.5.2.
    #: Padding every batch to a multiple of 128 avoids those lengths entirely.
    SAFE_PAD_MULTIPLE: int = 128

    def __init__(self, tokenizer: Any, pad_to_multiple_of: int | None = SAFE_PAD_MULTIPLE) -> None:
        if getattr(tokenizer, "padding_side", None) != "left":
            raise ValueError(
                f"JEVDataCollator requires tokenizer.padding_side == 'left' (got {getattr(tokenizer, 'padding_side', None)!r}); "
                "use training.modeling.load_tokenizer or set tokenizer.padding_side = 'left'"
            )
        if getattr(tokenizer, "pad_token_id", None) is None:
            raise ValueError("tokenizer has no pad token; set tokenizer.pad_token before collating")
        self.tokenizer = tokenizer
        self.pad_to_multiple_of = None if pad_to_multiple_of is None or int(pad_to_multiple_of) <= 1 else int(pad_to_multiple_of)
        # transformers >= 4.46 accepts an explicit padding_side in pad(); pass it when available
        try:
            self._pad_accepts_side = "padding_side" in inspect.signature(tokenizer.pad).parameters
        except (TypeError, ValueError):  # pragma: no cover - exotic tokenizer objects
            self._pad_accepts_side = False

    def _pad_prompts(self, features: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor]:
        if self.tokenizer.padding_side != "left":
            raise AssertionError("tokenizer.padding_side changed after collator construction; must be 'left'")
        encoded = {
            "input_ids": [list(f["input_ids"]) for f in features],
            "attention_mask": [list(f["attention_mask"]) for f in features],
        }
        kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {"padding": True, "pad_to_multiple_of": self.pad_to_multiple_of, "return_tensors": "pt"}
        if self._pad_accepts_side:
            kwargs["padding_side"] = "left"
        batch = self.tokenizer.pad(encoded, **kwargs)
        input_ids = torch.as_tensor(batch["input_ids"], dtype=torch.long)
        attention_mask = torch.as_tensor(batch["attention_mask"], dtype=torch.long)
        if input_ids.shape != attention_mask.shape or input_ids.ndim != 2:
            raise AssertionError(f"unexpected padded shapes {tuple(input_ids.shape)} / {tuple(attention_mask.shape)}")
        if input_ids.shape[0] and not bool(attention_mask[:, -1].all()):
            raise AssertionError("left padding violated: last attention column is not all ones")
        return input_ids, attention_mask

    def __call__(self, features: Sequence[Mapping[str, Any]]) -> dict[str, torch.Tensor]:
        if len(features) == 0:
            raise ValueError("cannot collate an empty batch")
        input_ids, attention_mask = self._pad_prompts(features)
        bsz = len(features)
        num_options = [int(f["num_options"]) for f in features]
        kmax = max(1, max(num_options))
        label_token_ids = torch.zeros(bsz, kmax, dtype=torch.long)
        label_mask = torch.zeros(bsz, kmax, dtype=torch.bool)
        target_dist = torch.zeros(bsz, kmax, dtype=torch.float32)
        perm = torch.full((bsz, kmax), -1, dtype=torch.long)
        target_idx = torch.empty(bsz, dtype=torch.long)
        qtype_id = torch.empty(bsz, dtype=torch.long)
        for b, f in enumerate(features):
            k = num_options[b]
            ids = list(f["label_token_ids"])
            dist = list(f["target_dist"])
            if len(ids) < k or len(dist) != k:
                raise ValueError(
                    f"example {f.get('example_id', b)}: num_options={k} but {len(ids)} label ids and {len(dist)} target entries"
                )
            label_token_ids[b, :k] = torch.as_tensor(ids[:k], dtype=torch.long)
            label_mask[b, :k] = True
            target_dist[b, :k] = torch.as_tensor(dist, dtype=torch.float32)
            p = list(f.get("perm") or range(k))
            if len(p) != k:
                raise ValueError(f"example {f.get('example_id', b)}: perm length {len(p)} != num_options {k}")
            perm[b, :k] = torch.as_tensor(p, dtype=torch.long)
            target_idx[b] = int(f["target_idx"])
            qtype_id[b] = int(f["qtype_id"])
        batch = {
            "input_ids": input_ids,
            "attention_mask": attention_mask,
            "label_token_ids": label_token_ids,
            "label_mask": label_mask,
            "target_dist": target_dist,
            "target_idx": target_idx,
            "qtype_id": qtype_id,
            "num_options": torch.as_tensor(num_options, dtype=torch.long),
            "perm": perm,
        }
        if any("weight" in f for f in features):
            batch["weight"] = torch.as_tensor([float(f.get("weight", 1.0)) for f in features], dtype=torch.float32)
        return batch

"""Configuration handling for SearchJev training.

The single source of hyper-parameters is ``config.yaml`` in the project root.
This module embeds the very same defaults so that a missing key never crashes,
loads the YAML file, applies the per-size preset, ``--set a.b=c`` overrides and
the convenience CLI flags, validates enums, resolves relative paths against the
project root and exposes a small attribute-accessible :class:`Config` object.

Resolution order (later wins)::

    embedded defaults -> config.yaml -> presets[model.size] -> --set overrides -> CLI flags

The preset is chosen from the *final* ``model.size`` (i.e. after taking
``--model``/``--debug``/``--set model.size=`` into account) so that
``--model 27B`` picks up the 27B batch-size preset while an explicit
``--set training.per_device_train_batch_size=2`` still wins over the preset.

This module is deliberately light: it imports neither torch nor transformers.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import copy
import logging
import os
import re
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any, Iterable, Mapping

import yaml

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Project layout
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#: Project root (``$P``): the parent directory of the ``training`` package.
PROJECT_ROOT: Path = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent

#: Default YAML file (``$P/config.yaml``).
DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH: Path = PROJECT_ROOT / "config.yaml"

# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Model size table
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

#: The ``base`` variant appends ``-Base`` (e.g. ``Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B-Base``).

    "0.8B": "Qwen/Qwen3.5-0.8B",

    "9B": "Qwen/Qwen3.5-9B",

#: and data.family_modes). Each family is a directory ``<data.root>/<family>/`` of per-dataset
#: jsonl files:

#: * ``unified_v2`` - SearchDecision-Bench (the converted search_jev_unified_v2 release; train/val/test);
#: * ``classification`` / ``ranking`` / ``search`` - SearchJev-V1 (validation only in the paper recipe).
FAMILIES: tuple[str, ...] = ("classification", "ranking", "search", "unified_v2")

class ConfigError(ValueError):
    """Raised for an invalid configuration value or override."""


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Config container
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


class Config(dict):
    """Attribute-accessible nested dict (``cfg.data.mode``).

    Nested mappings are wrapped recursively on construction and assignment, so
    ``cfg.training.learning_rate`` works at any depth.  :meth:`to_dict`
    converts back to plain ``dict`` objects (needed for YAML/JSON dumping).
    """

    __slots__ = ()

    def __init__(self, data: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None, **kwargs: Any) -> None:
        super().__init__()
        merged: dict[str, Any] = dict(data or {})
        merged.update(kwargs)
        for key, value in merged.items():
            self[key] = value

    # -- mapping protocol ---------------------------------------------------
    def __setitem__(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        super().__setitem__(key, _wrap(value))

    def __getattr__(self, key: str) -> Any:
        try:
            return self[key]
        except KeyError as exc:  # pragma: no cover - trivial
            raise AttributeError(key) from exc

    def __setattr__(self, key: str, value: Any) -> None:
        self[key] = value

    def __delattr__(self, key: str) -> None:
        try:
            del self[key]
        except KeyError as exc:  # pragma: no cover - trivial
            raise AttributeError(key) from exc

    def __deepcopy__(self, memo: dict[int, Any]) -> "Config":
        return Config(copy.deepcopy(self.to_dict(), memo))

    # -- conversion -----------------------------------------------------------
    def to_dict(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Return a plain, recursively converted ``dict`` copy."""
        return _unwrap(self)

    def to_yaml(self) -> str:
        """Render the configuration as a YAML string."""
        return yaml.safe_dump(self.to_dict(), sort_keys=False, allow_unicode=True, default_flow_style=False)

    def dump(self, path: str | os.PathLike[str]) -> Path:
        """Write the (resolved) configuration as YAML to ``path`` and return it."""
        out = Path(path)
        out.parent.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
        out.write_text(self.to_yaml(), encoding="utf-8")
        logger.info("Wrote resolved config to %s", out)
        return out

    # -- dotted access ------------------------------------------------------
    def get_path(self, dotted: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
        """Return the value at ``"a.b.c"`` or ``default`` if any level is missing."""
        node: Any = self
        for part in dotted.split("."):
            if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or part not in node:
                return default
            node = node[part]
        return node

    def set_path(self, dotted: str, value: Any) -> None:
        """Set the value at ``"a.b.c"``, creating intermediate mappings."""
        _set_dotted(self, dotted, value)


def _wrap(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, Config):
        return value
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return Config(value)
    if isinstance(value, list):
        return [_wrap(v) for v in value]
    return value


def _unwrap(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, Mapping):
        return {k: _unwrap(v) for k, v in value.items()}
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [_unwrap(v) for v in value]
    return value


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Merging / dotted helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def deep_merge(base: dict[str, Any], update: Mapping[str, Any] | None) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Recursively merge ``update`` into ``base`` **in place** and return ``base``.

    Mappings are merged key by key; any other value (including lists) replaces
    the previous one.  A ``None`` in ``update`` is a real value (it clears the
    key), which is how ``max_train_per_dataset: null`` works.
    """
    if not update:
        return base
    for key, value in update.items():
        if isinstance(value, Mapping) and isinstance(base.get(key), Mapping):
            base[key] = deep_merge(dict(base[key]), value)
        else:
            base[key] = copy.deepcopy(_unwrap(value)) if isinstance(value, (Mapping, list)) else value
    return base


def _set_dotted(node: Any, dotted: str, value: Any) -> None:
    parts = dotted.split(".")
    for part in parts[:-1]:
        if part not in node or not isinstance(node[part], Mapping):
            node[part] = {} if not isinstance(node, Config) else Config()
        node = node[part]
    node[parts[-1]] = value


def _get_dotted(node: Any, dotted: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
    for part in dotted.split("."):
        if not isinstance(node, Mapping) or part not in node:
            return default
        node = node[part]
    return node


def parse_override(item: str) -> tuple[str, Any]:
    """Parse one ``a.b.c=value`` override; the value is YAML-parsed.

    ``training.max_steps=100`` -> ``("training.max_steps", 100)``;
    ``data.families=[classification,search]`` -> list;
    ``data.mode=full`` -> ``"full"``; ``x=null`` -> ``None``.
    """
    if "=" not in item:
        raise ConfigError(f"override must look like a.b=c, got {item!r}")
    key, _, raw = item.partition("=")
    key = key.strip()
    if not key or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Za-z0-9_.\-]+", key):
        raise ConfigError(f"invalid override key {key!r} in {item!r}")
    raw = raw.strip()
    try:
        value = yaml.safe_load(raw) if raw != "" else ""
    except yaml.YAMLError:
        value = raw
    # YAML 1.1 reads "no"/"yes"/"on"/"off" as booleans and "4" as an int; keep the
    # literal text for string-valued keys (training.eval_strategy=no,
    # distributed.num_gpus=4 -> normalised later in _finalize).
    default = _get_dotted(DEFAULTS, key, default=None)
    if isinstance(default, str) and value is not None and not isinstance(value, str):
        value = raw
    return key, value


def parse_overrides(items: Iterable[str] | None) -> list[tuple[str, Any]]:
    """Parse a sequence of ``a.b=c`` strings (see :func:`parse_override`)."""
    return [parse_override(it) for it in (items or [])]


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Model size helpers
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------

_SIZE_RE = re.compile(r"(?i)(?:^|[^0-9.])(0\.8|2|4|9|27)\s*b(?:$|[^a-z0-9])")


def try_normalize_size(value: Any) -> str | None:
    """Return the canonical size key (``"4B"``) for ``4b``/``Qwen3.5-4B``/... or ``None``."""
    if value is None:
        return None
    text = str(value).strip()
    if not text:
        return None
    direct = text.upper()
    if direct in MODEL_SIZES:
        return direct
    # Accept things like "qwen3.5-4b", "Qwen/Qwen3.5-0.8B-Base", "4b", "27B-instruct".
    match = _SIZE_RE.search(" " + text + " ")
    if match:
        key = match.group(1) + "B"
        if key in MODEL_SIZES:
            return key
    return None


def normalize_size(value: Any) -> str:
    """Like :func:`try_normalize_size` but raises :class:`ConfigError` when unknown."""
    key = try_normalize_size(value)
    if key is None:
        raise ConfigError(f"unknown model size {value!r}; expected one of {list(MODEL_SIZES)}")
    return key


def resolve_model_name(cfg: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Return the HF id / local path of the base model for ``cfg``.

    ``model.name`` (explicit id or path) wins; otherwise the size+variant table
    is used: ``Qwen/Qwen3.5-<size>`` for ``instruct`` and ``...-Base`` for ``base``.
    """
    model = cfg.get("model", {})
    name = model.get("name")
    if name:
        return str(name)
    size = normalize_size(model.get("size", "4B"))
    variant = str(model.get("variant", "instruct")).lower()
    if variant not in ENUMS["model.variant"]:
        raise ConfigError(f"model.variant must be one of {ENUMS['model.variant']}, got {variant!r}")
    base = MODEL_SIZES[size]
    return base + "-Base" if variant == "base" else base


def effective_batch_size(cfg: Mapping[str, Any], world_size: int = 1) -> int:
    """Global optimisation batch size = per-device batch x grad-accum x world size."""
    training = cfg.get("training", {})
    per_device = int(training.get("per_device_train_batch_size", 1))
    accum = int(training.get("gradient_accumulation_steps", 1))
    return per_device * accum * max(1, int(world_size))


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
# Loading
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------------


def _read_yaml(path: Path) -> dict[str, Any]:
    with open(path, "r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        loaded = yaml.safe_load(fh)
    if loaded is None:
        return {}
    if not isinstance(loaded, Mapping):
        raise ConfigError(f"{path}: top level must be a mapping, got {type(loaded).__name__}")
    unknown = sorted(set(loaded) - set(DEFAULTS))
    if unknown:
        logger.warning("%s: unknown top-level keys %s (kept, but unused)", path, unknown)
    return dict(loaded)


def _split_list(value: Any) -> Any:
    """Turn ``"a,b"`` into ``["a", "b"]``; lists pass through; ``None`` stays ``None``."""
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, str):
        return [v.strip() for v in value.split(",") if v.strip()]
    if isinstance(value, (list, tuple)):
        return [str(v).strip() if isinstance(v, str) else v for v in value]
    return value


def _coerce_scalars(node: dict[str, Any], defaults: Mapping[str, Any], prefix: str = "") -> None:
    """Coerce string values to the numeric/bool type of the matching default.

    Handles YAML quirks such as ``5e-5`` (parsed as a string by YAML 1.1) and
    ``--set training.bf16=false`` when the caller passed the raw string.
    """
    for key, default in defaults.items():
        if key not in node:
            continue
        value = node[key]
        path = f"{prefix}{key}"
        if isinstance(default, Mapping):
            if isinstance(value, Mapping):
                if not isinstance(value, dict):
                    value = dict(value)
                    node[key] = value
                _coerce_scalars(value, default, prefix=path + ".")
            continue
        if isinstance(default, list) and isinstance(value, str):
            node[key] = _split_list(value)
            continue
        if not isinstance(value, str):
            continue
        text = value.strip()
        if isinstance(default, bool):
            low = text.lower()
            if low in ("true", "yes", "on", "1"):
                node[key] = True
            elif low in ("false", "no", "off", "0"):
                node[key] = False
            else:
                raise ConfigError(f"{path}: expected a boolean, got {value!r}")
        elif isinstance(default, int) and not isinstance(default, bool):
            try:
                node[key] = int(text)
            except ValueError:
                try:
                    node[key] = float(text)
                except ValueError:
                    raise ConfigError(f"{path}: expected a number, got {value!r}") from None
        elif isinstance(default, float):
            try:
                node[key] = float(text)
            except ValueError:
                raise ConfigError(f"{path}: expected a number, got {value!r}") from None


def _effective_size(cfg: Mapping[str, Any], overrides: list[tuple[str, Any]], cli: Mapping[str, Any]) -> str:
    """Determine the final ``model.size`` before presets are applied."""
    size: Any = _get_dotted(cfg, "model.size", "4B")
    for key, value in overrides:
        if key == "model.size":
            size = value
    cli_model = cli.get("model")
    if cli.get("debug") and cli_model is None and "model.size" not in {k for k, _ in overrides}:
        size = DEBUG_OVERRIDES["model"]["size"]
    if cli_model is not None and try_normalize_size(cli_model) is not None:
        size = cli_model
    return normalize_size(size)


def _flatten_keys(node: Mapping[str, Any], prefix: str = "") -> list[str]:
    """Dotted leaf keys of a nested mapping."""
    out: list[str] = []
    for key, value in node.items():
        path = f"{prefix}{key}"
        if isinstance(value, Mapping):
            out.extend(_flatten_keys(value, path + "."))
        else:
            out.append(path)
    return out


def _apply_cli(cfg: dict[str, Any], cli: Mapping[str, Any], explicit: Iterable[str] = ()) -> None:
    """Apply the convenience CLI flags (``--model``, ``--full``, ...).

    ``explicit`` lists the dotted keys given via ``--set``; the ``--debug``
    preset never overrides one of those (``--debug --set training.max_steps=8``
    runs 8 steps), while the other debug values still apply.
    """
    explicit = set(explicit)
    model = cli.get("model")
    if model is not None:
        size = try_normalize_size(model)
        if size is not None and "/" not in str(model) and not os.path.exists(str(model)):
            _set_dotted(cfg, "model.size", size)
            _set_dotted(cfg, "model.name", None)
        else:
            # An explicit HF id or local path.
            _set_dotted(cfg, "model.name", str(model))
            if size is not None:
                _set_dotted(cfg, "model.size", size)
    if cli.get("variant") is not None:
        _set_dotted(cfg, "model.variant", str(cli["variant"]).lower())
        explicit.add("model.variant")
    if cli.get("full"):
        _set_dotted(cfg, "data.mode", "full")
    if cli.get("families") is not None:
        _set_dotted(cfg, "data.families", _split_list(cli["families"]))
    if cli.get("gpus") is not None:
        gpus = cli["gpus"]
        if isinstance(gpus, (list, tuple)):
            gpus = ",".join(str(g) for g in gpus)
        _set_dotted(cfg, "distributed.gpus", str(gpus))
    if cli.get("num_gpus") is not None:
        _set_dotted(cfg, "distributed.num_gpus", cli["num_gpus"])
        explicit.add("distributed.num_gpus")
    if cli.get("gpus") is not None:
        explicit.add("distributed.num_gpus")  # explicit ids win over the debug 1-GPU default
    if cli.get("wandb"):
        _set_dotted(cfg, "run.report_to", "wandb")
        _set_dotted(cfg, "run.wandb_project", str(cli["wandb"]))
    if cli.get("name") is not None:
        _set_dotted(cfg, "run.name", str(cli["name"]))
    if cli.get("resume") is not None:
        _set_dotted(cfg, "training.resume_from_checkpoint", str(cli["resume"]))
    if cli.get("debug"):
        for dotted in _flatten_keys(DEBUG_OVERRIDES):
            if dotted in explicit or (dotted.startswith("model.") and cli.get("model") is not None):
                continue  # an explicit --set / --model wins over the debug preset
            _set_dotted(cfg, dotted, _get_dotted(DEBUG_OVERRIDES, dotted))
        name = str(_get_dotted(cfg, "run.name", "jev"))
        if not name.endswith("-debug"):
            _set_dotted(cfg, "run.name", name + "-debug")


def _normalize_family_modes(value: Any) -> dict[str, str]:
    """Validate ``data.family_modes`` (``{family: sampled|full}``; ``None`` -> ``{}``)."""
    if value in (None, "", "null", "None", "none"):
        return {}
    if not isinstance(value, Mapping):
        raise ConfigError(f"data.family_modes must be a mapping family -> sampled|full, got {value!r}")
    out: dict[str, str] = {}
    for fam, mode in value.items():
        fam = str(fam).strip()
        if fam not in FAMILIES:
            raise ConfigError(f"data.family_modes: unknown family {fam!r}; allowed {list(FAMILIES)}")
        mode = str(mode).strip().lower()
        if mode not in ENUMS["data.mode"]:
            raise ConfigError(f"data.family_modes.{fam} must be one of {ENUMS['data.mode']}, got {mode!r}")
        out[fam] = mode
    return out


def family_mode(cfg: Mapping[str, Any], family: str) -> str:
    """Effective source mode of ``family``: ``data.family_modes[family]`` or else ``data.mode``.

    It only decides which source files are read (``sampled_300k/<family>_300k.jsonl`` vs every
    per-dataset file); the processed cache stays at ``<processed_dir>/<data.mode>/<family>``.
    """
    data = cfg.get("data", {}) if isinstance(cfg, Mapping) else {}
    modes = data.get("family_modes") or {}
    return str(modes.get(family) or data.get("mode") or "sampled").lower()


def prepared_families(cfg: Mapping[str, Any]) -> list[str]:
    """Families ``prepare_data`` handles: ``data.families`` (train+val) then ``data.val_only_families`` (val only)."""
    data = cfg.get("data", {}) if isinstance(cfg, Mapping) else {}
    out = list(data.get("families") or [])
    return out + [f for f in (data.get("val_only_families") or []) if f not in out]


def _resolve_path(value: str) -> str:
    path = Path(os.path.expanduser(str(value)))
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = PROJECT_ROOT / path
    return str(path)


def _finalize(cfg: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Normalise, validate and resolve paths in place."""
    _coerce_scalars(cfg, DEFAULTS)

    # model
    model = cfg["model"]
    model["size"] = normalize_size(model.get("size", "4B"))
    model["variant"] = str(model.get("variant", "instruct")).lower()
    if model.get("name") in ("", "null", "None"):
        model["name"] = None
    if model.get("name"):
        candidate = Path(os.path.expanduser(str(model["name"])))
        if not candidate.is_absolute() and (PROJECT_ROOT / candidate).exists():
            candidate = PROJECT_ROOT / candidate
        if candidate.exists():
            model["name"] = str(candidate.resolve())

    # lists that may come as comma-separated strings
    data = cfg["data"]
    data["families"] = _split_list(data.get("families")) or []
    data["val_only_families"] = _split_list(data.get("val_only_families")) or []
    data["family_modes"] = _normalize_family_modes(data.get("family_modes"))
    data["train_splits"] = _split_list(data.get("train_splits")) or []
    data["exclude_datasets"] = _split_list(data.get("exclude_datasets")) or []
    data["include_datasets"] = _split_list(data.get("include_datasets"))
    cfg["lora"]["target_modules"] = _split_list(cfg["lora"].get("target_modules")) or []
    if isinstance(cfg["labels"].get("alphabet"), (list, tuple)):
        cfg["labels"]["alphabet"] = [str(a) for a in cfg["labels"]["alphabet"]]
    if cfg["distributed"].get("gpus") is not None:
        gpus = cfg["distributed"]["gpus"]
        if isinstance(gpus, (list, tuple)):
            gpus = ",".join(str(g) for g in gpus)
        cfg["distributed"]["gpus"] = str(gpus).replace(" ", "")
        if cfg["distributed"]["gpus"].lower() in ("", "null", "none", "all"):
            cfg["distributed"]["gpus"] = None
    dist = cfg["distributed"]
    num = dist.get("num_gpus", "all")
    if isinstance(num, bool):
        raise ConfigError("distributed.num_gpus must be 'all' or a positive integer")
    if num is None or (isinstance(num, str) and num.strip().lower() in ("", "all", "auto", "null", "none", "max")):
        dist["num_gpus"] = "all"
    else:
        try:
            n_gpus = int(num)
        except (TypeError, ValueError):
            raise ConfigError(f"distributed.num_gpus must be 'all' or a positive integer, got {num!r}") from None
        if n_gpus < 1:
            raise ConfigError("distributed.num_gpus must be >= 1 (or 'all')")
        dist["num_gpus"] = n_gpus
    if dist.get("min_free_gb") in ("", "null", "None", "none"):
        dist["min_free_gb"] = None
    if dist.get("min_free_gb") is not None:
        dist["min_free_gb"] = float(dist["min_free_gb"])
    dist["gpu_selection"] = str(dist.get("gpu_selection") or "free").lower()
    for section in ("calibration", "evaluation"):
        node = cfg.setdefault(section, {})
        max_examples = node.get("max_examples", DEFAULTS[section]["max_examples"])
        if max_examples in ("", "null", "None", "none"):
            max_examples = None
        node["max_examples"] = None if max_examples is None else int(max_examples)
        if node["max_examples"] is not None and node["max_examples"] < 1:
            raise ConfigError(f"{section}.max_examples must be >= 1 or null")
    lora_cfg = cfg["lora"]
    if lora_cfg.get("init_adapter") in ("", "null", "None", "none"):
        lora_cfg["init_adapter"] = None
    if lora_cfg.get("init_adapter"):
        adapter_path = Path(os.path.expanduser(str(lora_cfg["init_adapter"])))
        if not adapter_path.is_absolute():
            adapter_path = PROJECT_ROOT / adapter_path
        if not adapter_path.exists():
            raise ConfigError(f"lora.init_adapter not found: {adapter_path}")
        lora_cfg["init_adapter"] = str(adapter_path.resolve())

    # enums
    for dotted, allowed in ENUMS.items():
        value = _get_dotted(cfg, dotted)
        if value is None:
            continue
        if isinstance(value, str):
            value = value.lower() if dotted != "model.attn_implementation" else value
            _set_dotted(cfg, dotted, value)
        if value not in allowed:
            raise ConfigError(f"{dotted} must be one of {allowed}, got {value!r}")

    alphabet = cfg["labels"].get("alphabet")
    if isinstance(alphabet, str):
        if alphabet not in ("basic", "extended"):
            raise ConfigError(f"labels.alphabet must be 'basic', 'extended' or a list, got {alphabet!r}")
    elif not isinstance(alphabet, list) or not alphabet:
        raise ConfigError("labels.alphabet must be 'basic', 'extended' or a non-empty list of labels")

    unknown_families = [f for f in data["families"] if f not in FAMILIES]
    if unknown_families:
        raise ConfigError(f"data.families contains unknown families {unknown_families}; allowed {list(FAMILIES)}")
    if not data["families"]:
        raise ConfigError("data.families must not be empty")
    unknown_val_only = [f for f in data["val_only_families"] if f not in FAMILIES]
    if unknown_val_only:
        raise ConfigError(f"data.val_only_families contains unknown families {unknown_val_only}; allowed {list(FAMILIES)}")
    overlap = [f for f in data["val_only_families"] if f in data["families"]]
    if overlap:
        raise ConfigError(
            f"data.val_only_families {overlap} are also in data.families {data['families']}; a family is either "
            "trained on (train+val) or val-only - e.g. --set data.val_only_families=[] when training on them"
        )
    if "test" in data["train_splits"]:
        raise ConfigError("data.train_splits must never contain 'test'")
    if data.get("val_split") == "test":
        raise ConfigError("data.val_split must not be 'test'")
    if data.get("val_split") in data["train_splits"]:
        raise ConfigError("data.val_split must not also be in data.train_splits")

    perm = data.get("permutation") or {}
    if not isinstance(perm, Mapping):
        raise ConfigError("data.permutation must be a mapping")
    copies = int(perm.get("copies", 1) or 1)
    if copies < 1:
        raise ConfigError("data.permutation.copies must be >= 1")
    perm = dict(perm)
    perm["copies"] = copies
    for qtype in ("choice", "noul", "score"):
        perm[qtype] = bool(perm.get(qtype, False))
    data["permutation"] = perm

    training = cfg["training"]
    for key in ("per_device_train_batch_size", "per_device_eval_batch_size", "gradient_accumulation_steps"):
        if int(training.get(key, 1)) < 1:
            raise ConfigError(f"training.{key} must be >= 1")
    if float(training.get("learning_rate", 0)) <= 0:
        raise ConfigError("training.learning_rate must be > 0")
    if not 0.0 <= float(training.get("warmup_ratio", 0)) <= 1.0:
        raise ConfigError("training.warmup_ratio must be in [0, 1]")
    if training.get("resume_from_checkpoint") in ("", "null", "None", "none"):
        training["resume_from_checkpoint"] = None

    sw = cfg["loss"].get("sample_weights")
    if sw in ("", "null", "None", "none", {}, False):
        sw = None
    if sw is not None:
        if not isinstance(sw, Mapping):
            raise ConfigError(f"loss.sample_weights must be a mapping (e.g. {{weak: 0.5, label_tier: {{llm_teacher: 0.5}}}}) or null, got {sw!r}")
        clean: dict[str, Any] = {}
        for key, rule in sw.items():
            if isinstance(rule, Mapping):
                clean[str(key)] = {str(v): float(w) for v, w in rule.items()}
                bad = [w for w in clean[str(key)].values() if not 0.0 <= w <= 10.0]
            else:
                clean[str(key)] = float(rule)
                bad = [] if 0.0 <= clean[str(key)] <= 10.0 else [clean[str(key)]]
            if bad:
                raise ConfigError(f"loss.sample_weights.{key}: weights must be in [0, 10], got {bad}")
        sw = clean
    cfg["loss"]["sample_weights"] = sw
    lam = float(cfg["loss"].get("lambda_brier", 0.0))
    if lam < 0:
        raise ConfigError("loss.lambda_brier must be >= 0")
    frac = float(cfg["calibration"].get("fraction", 0.5))
    if not 0.0 < frac < 1.0:
        raise ConfigError("calibration.fraction must be strictly between 0 and 1")
    if cfg["labels"].get("max_options") is not None and int(cfg["labels"]["max_options"]) < 2:
        raise ConfigError("labels.max_options must be >= 2 (or null)")
    if int(cfg["prompt"].get("max_length", 0)) < 64:
        raise ConfigError("prompt.max_length is unreasonably small (< 64)")
    r = int(cfg["lora"].get("r", 0))
    if cfg["lora"].get("enabled") and r < 1:
        raise ConfigError("lora.r must be >= 1 when lora.enabled")

    # paths
    for dotted in PATH_KEYS:
        value = _get_dotted(cfg, dotted)
        if value is not None:
            _set_dotted(cfg, dotted, _resolve_path(value))
    run = cfg["run"]
    hf_home = run.get("hf_home")
    run["hf_home"] = None if hf_home in (None, "", "null", "None", "none") else str(Path(os.path.expanduser(str(hf_home))))
    run["name"] = str(run.get("name") or DEFAULTS["run"]["name"]).strip().replace("{size}", str(model["size"]).lower())
    if not run["name"] or "/" in run["name"] or run["name"] in (".", ".."):
        raise ConfigError(f"run.name must be a plain directory name, got {run['name']!r}")
    run["output_dir"] = str(Path(run["output_root"]) / run["name"])
    run["seed"] = int(run.get("seed", 42))
    run["model_name"] = resolve_model_name(cfg)


def load_config(
    path: str | os.PathLike[str] | None = None,
    overrides: Iterable[str] = (),
    cli: Mapping[str, Any] | None = None,
) -> Config:
    """Load, merge, validate and resolve the configuration.

    Args:
        path: YAML file; ``None`` uses ``$P/config.yaml`` when it exists
            (embedded defaults alone otherwise).
        overrides: ``["a.b=c", ...]`` strings from ``--set`` (YAML-parsed values).
        cli: convenience flags; recognised keys are ``model``, ``variant``,
            ``full``, ``families``, ``gpus``, ``name``, ``resume`` and ``debug``.

    Returns:
        A :class:`Config` with, in addition to the YAML keys, ``run.output_dir``
        (``output_root/name``) and ``run.model_name`` (resolved base model).
    """
    cfg: dict[str, Any] = copy.deepcopy(DEFAULTS)

    yaml_path: Path | None
    if path is None:
        yaml_path = DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH if DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH.exists() else None
    else:
        yaml_path = Path(path).expanduser()
        if not yaml_path.is_absolute() and not yaml_path.exists():
            yaml_path = PROJECT_ROOT / yaml_path
        if not yaml_path.exists():
            raise ConfigError(f"config file not found: {path}")
    if yaml_path is not None:
        logger.info("Loading config from %s", yaml_path)
        deep_merge(cfg, _read_yaml(yaml_path))
    else:
        logger.info("No config.yaml found; using embedded defaults")

    cli = dict(cli or {})
    parsed = parse_overrides(overrides)

    # presets for the final model size (deep-merge over defaults+yaml)
    size = _effective_size(cfg, parsed, cli)
    presets = cfg.get("presets") or {}
    preset = None
    for key, value in presets.items():
        if try_normalize_size(key) == size:
            preset = value
            break
    if preset:
        logger.info("Applying preset for model size %s: %s", size, preset)
        deep_merge(cfg, preset)
    _set_dotted(cfg, "model.size", size)

    # --set overrides
    for key, value in parsed:
        current = _get_dotted(cfg, key, default=None)
        if isinstance(current, list) and isinstance(value, str):
            value = _split_list(value)
        _set_dotted(cfg, key, value)

    # convenience CLI flags (win over everything; --debug does not touch --set keys)
    _apply_cli(cfg, cli, explicit=[key for key, _ in parsed])

    _finalize(cfg)
    return Config(cfg)


__all__ = [
    "Config",
    "ConfigError",
    "DEFAULTS",
    "DEBUG_OVERRIDES",
    "DEFAULT_CONFIG_PATH",
    "ENUMS",
    "FAMILIES",
    "LEGACY_FAMILIES",
    "MODEL_SIZES",
    "family_mode",
    "prepared_families",
    "PROJECT_ROOT",
    "deep_merge",
    "effective_batch_size",
    "load_config",
    "normalize_size",
    "parse_override",
    "parse_overrides",
    "resolve_model_name",
    "try_normalize_size",
]



================================================

"""Data pipeline for SearchJev.

This module covers everything between the raw JEV-format jsonl files (SearchJev-V1/V2) and
the tensors consumed by the trainer:

* **source discovery** (:func:`list_sources`, :func:`full_source_files`):
  ``sampled`` mode uses the ``sampled_300k/<family>_300k.jsonl`` subsets,
  ``full`` mode uses every per-dataset ``<family>/*.jsonl`` file.  The mode is
  chosen per family: ``data.family_modes[family]`` if set, else ``data.mode``
  (e.g. ``unified_v2`` has no sampled subset and runs ``full``
  while the SearchJev-V1 families stay ``sampled``).  The processed cache of every
  family stays at ``<processed_dir>/<data.mode>/<family>`` (``data.mode`` = the
  global mode); the per-family source mode is part of the family's cache key.
* **val-only families** (``data.val_only_families``): prepared exactly like the
  training families (same cache), but only their ``val.jsonl`` is used (validation,
  in-training eval, calibration and held-out evaluation); their ``train.jsonl`` is
  never trained on.
* **prepare** (:func:`prepare_data`): a streaming, memory-light conversion of
  JEV rows into :class:`~training.schema.DecisionExample` json lines, split
  into ``train.jsonl`` / ``val.jsonl`` per family.  Rows whose ``split`` is
  ``test`` are never written.  The work is distributed over a
  ``multiprocessing.Pool`` (one task per source file); per-dataset caps are
  enforced with hash-keyed reservoirs so the output is deterministic no
  matter how the pool schedules the files.  Families without any ``val``
  rows in their sources (the ranking 300k subset) fall back to streaming a
  small number of ``val`` rows from the family's full per-dataset files.
* **dataset** (:class:`JEVDataset`): an offset-indexed jsonl reader (index
  cached at ``<path>.idx.npy``) with lazily opened per-process file handles,
  deterministic option permutation and deterministic sub-sampling.
* helpers used by the scripts: :func:`build_train_dataset`,
  :func:`build_val_dataset`, :func:`split_val_for_calibration`,
  :func:`read_examples`, :func:`format_prepare_table`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import enum
import hashlib
import heapq
import json
import logging
import multiprocessing as mp
import os
import random
import shutil
import time
from collections.abc import Iterable, Iterator, Mapping, Sequence
from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import numpy as np
from torch.utils.data import ConcatDataset, Dataset

from .labels import LabelSpace
from .prompting import PromptBuilder
from .schema import DecisionExample, example_weight, jev_row_to_examples, render_state

try:  # the policy type lives in schema.py; string names are accepted as well
    from .schema import OverLimitPolicy  # type: ignore[attr-defined]
except ImportError:  # pragma: no cover - depends on the schema module's API
    OverLimitPolicy = None  # type: ignore[assignment]

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

__all__ = [
    "QTYPES",
    "QTYPE_IDS",
    "PREPARE_VERSION",
    "PreparedData",
    "JEVDataset",
    "list_sources",
    "full_source_files",
    "prepare_data",
    "build_train_dataset",
    "build_val_dataset",
    "split_val_for_calibration",
    "calibration_unit",
    "read_examples",
    "count_lines",
    "load_or_build_line_index",
    "format_prepare_table",
    "cache_status",
]

#: Question types in canonical order and their integer ids (used by the
#: trainer / metrics to group examples).
QTYPES: tuple[str, ...] = ("choice", "noul", "score")
QTYPE_IDS: dict[str, int] = {"choice": 0, "noul": 1, "score": 2}

#: Bump when the on-disk format of processed files changes; part of the cache key.
PREPARE_VERSION = 2

_PROJECT_ROOT = Path(__file__).resolve().parent.parent
_SPLITS: tuple[str, str] = ("train", "val")
_TEST_SPLIT = "test"
_INDEX_SUFFIX = ".idx.npy"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Small helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _cfg_get(cfg: Any, dotted: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
    """Read ``a.b.c`` from an attribute-accessible nested dict (or plain dicts)."""
    node = cfg
    for part in dotted.split("."):
        if node is None:
            return default
        if isinstance(node, Mapping):
            if part not in node:
                return default
            node = node[part]
        elif hasattr(node, part):
            node = getattr(node, part)
        else:
            return default
    return default if node is None else node


def _resolve_path(p: str | os.PathLike[str]) -> Path:
    """Resolve a possibly relative path against the project root."""
    path = Path(p).expanduser()
    if not path.is_absolute():
        path = _PROJECT_ROOT / path
    return path


def _stable_hash(text: str) -> int:
    """Deterministic 64-bit hash of a string (first 8 bytes of sha1)."""
    return int.from_bytes(hashlib.sha1(text.encode("utf-8")).digest()[:8], "big")


def _hash_unit(text: str) -> float:
    """Deterministic uniform number in ``[0, 1)`` derived from ``text``."""
    return _stable_hash(text) / float(1 << 64)


def _as_name_set(value: Any) -> frozenset[str] | None:
    """Normalise ``include/exclude_datasets`` config values to a frozenset."""
    if value is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(value, str):
        items = [v.strip() for v in value.split(",")]
    else:
        items = [str(v).strip() for v in value]
    items = [v for v in items if v]
    return frozenset(items) if items else None


def _resolve_policy(name: str | None) -> Any:
    """Map the config string to the schema module's ``OverLimitPolicy`` (if it is an Enum)."""
    if name is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(name, enum.Enum):
        return name
    text = str(name).strip().lower()
    if OverLimitPolicy is not None and isinstance(OverLimitPolicy, type) and issubclass(OverLimitPolicy, enum.Enum):
        try:
            return OverLimitPolicy(text)
        except ValueError:
            for member in OverLimitPolicy:  # match by member name as a fallback
                if member.name.lower() == text:
                    return member
            raise ValueError(f"unknown over_limit_policy {name!r}; valid: {[m.value for m in OverLimitPolicy]}")
    return text


def _label_space_max_options(label_space: LabelSpace | None) -> int | None:
    return None if label_space is None else int(getattr(label_space, "max_options"))


def _to_jsonable(value: Any) -> Any:
    if isinstance(value, Path):
        return str(value)
    if isinstance(value, (frozenset, set, tuple)):
        return sorted(str(v) for v in value)
    if isinstance(value, enum.Enum):
        return value.value
    return value


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Source discovery
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def full_source_files(root: str | os.PathLike[str], family: str) -> list[Path]:
    """All per-dataset jsonl files of ``root/family`` (sorted), excluding ``all_*`` files.

    The ``sampled_300k`` directory is not a direct child file, so it is naturally excluded.
    """
    fam_dir = _resolve_path(root) / family
    if not fam_dir.is_dir():
        raise FileNotFoundError(f"family directory not found: {fam_dir}")
    return sorted(p for p in fam_dir.glob("*.jsonl") if p.is_file() and not p.name.startswith("all_"))


def list_sources(root: str | os.PathLike[str], family: str, mode: str) -> list[Path]:
    """Source files for ``family`` in the given data ``mode`` (``sampled`` | ``full``)."""
    mode = str(mode).lower()
    if mode == "sampled":
        path = _resolve_path(root) / family / "sampled_300k" / f"{family}_300k.jsonl"
        if not path.is_file():
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"sampled source not found: {path}")
        return [path]
    if mode == "full":
        files = full_source_files(root, family)
        if not files:
            raise FileNotFoundError(f"no per-dataset jsonl files under {_resolve_path(root) / family}")
        return files
    raise ValueError(f"unknown data mode {mode!r} (expected 'sampled' or 'full')")


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Prepare: per-source-file worker
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _FileTask:
    """One unit of prepare work: a single source file of one family."""

    family: str
    source: str
    part_dir: str
    part_index: int
    train_splits: tuple[str, ...]
    val_split: str
    include: frozenset[str] | None
    exclude: frozenset[str]
    max_train: int | None
    max_val: int | None
    val_only_quota: int | None = None  # fallback mode: collect at most this many val rows per dataset
    default_dataset: str = ""


@dataclass
class _FileResult:
    """What a worker hands back to the parent process (small: no example content)."""

    part_index: int
    source: str
    uncapped_parts: dict[str, str | None]
    capped_parts: dict[str, str | None]
    capped_meta: dict[str, list[tuple[int, str, str, int]]]  # split -> [(hash, dataset, qtype, K)] in file order
    stats: dict[str, Any]
    seconds: float


# Pool workers receive the label space through the initializer (no pickling of
# the tokenizer through the task queue; with the default ``fork`` start method
# nothing is pickled at all).
_WORKER_LABEL_SPACE: LabelSpace | None = None
_WORKER_POLICY: Any = None


def _init_worker(label_space: LabelSpace | None, policy: Any) -> None:
    global _WORKER_LABEL_SPACE, _WORKER_POLICY
    _WORKER_LABEL_SPACE = label_space
    _WORKER_POLICY = policy
    os.environ.setdefault("TOKENIZERS_PARALLELISM", "false")


def _new_stats() -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {
        "rows_read": 0,
        "rows_by_split": {"train": 0, "val": 0, "test": 0, "other": 0},
        "rows_malformed": 0,
        "rows_excluded_by_dataset": 0,
        "rows_adapter_error": 0,
        "examples": {"train": 0, "val": 0},
        "capped_candidates": {"train": 0, "val": 0},
        "per_dataset": {},
        "per_qtype": {"train": {}, "val": {}},
        "n_options_hist": {"train": {}, "val": {}},
        "drops": {},
    }


def _stats_add_example(stats: dict[str, Any], split: str, dataset: str, qtype: str, k: int) -> None:
    stats["examples"][split] += 1
    ds = stats["per_dataset"].setdefault(dataset, {"train": 0, "val": 0})
    ds[split] += 1
    qt = stats["per_qtype"][split]
    qt[qtype] = qt.get(qtype, 0) + 1
    hist = stats["n_options_hist"][split]
    hist[str(k)] = hist.get(str(k), 0) + 1


def _merge_counts(dst: dict[str, Any], src: dict[str, Any]) -> None:
    """Recursively add integer counters of ``src`` into ``dst``."""
    for key, value in src.items():
        if isinstance(value, dict):
            _merge_counts(dst.setdefault(key, {}), value)
        elif isinstance(value, (int, float)) and not isinstance(value, bool):
            dst[key] = dst.get(key, 0) + value
        else:
            dst.setdefault(key, value)


def _process_source(task: _FileTask) -> _FileResult:
    """Stream one source file and write its train/val candidates as part files.

    Uncapped datasets are written directly (``part-<n>.<split>.jsonl``).  For
    datasets with a per-dataset cap a bounded reservoir keeps the ``cap``
    examples with the smallest ``sha1(example.id)``; they are written to
    ``part-<n>.<split>.capped.jsonl`` together with their hashes so that the
    parent process can perform the *global* selection deterministically.
    """
    t0 = time.time()
    label_space = _WORKER_LABEL_SPACE
    policy = _WORKER_POLICY
    source = Path(task.source)
    part_dir = Path(task.part_dir)
    part_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    stats = _new_stats()
    caps = {"train": task.max_train, "val": task.max_val}
    writers: dict[str, Any] = {}
    uncapped_parts: dict[str, str | None] = {"train": None, "val": None}
    capped_parts: dict[str, str | None] = {"train": None, "val": None}
    reservoirs: dict[tuple[str, str], list[tuple[int, str, str, tuple[str, str, int]]]] = {}
    fallback_rows: dict[str, int] = {}
    fallback_datasets_seen: set[str] = set()
    quota = task.val_only_quota
    train_splits = set(task.train_splits)
    warned = 0

    def writer_for(split: str):
        fh = writers.get(split)
        if fh is None:
            path = part_dir / f"part-{task.part_index:05d}.{split}.jsonl"
            fh = open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8")
            writers[split] = fh
            uncapped_parts[split] = str(path)
        return fh

    try:
        with open(source, "r", encoding="utf-8", errors="replace") as fin:
            for raw in fin:
                if not raw.strip():
                    continue
                stats["rows_read"] += 1
                try:
                    row = json.loads(raw)
                    if not isinstance(row, dict):
                        raise ValueError("row is not a JSON object")
                except ValueError:
                    stats["rows_malformed"] += 1
                    continue
                split = str(row.get("split", "") or "")
                if split == _TEST_SPLIT:
                    stats["rows_by_split"]["test"] += 1
                    continue  # test rows are never used for training or validation
                if quota is not None:
                    # fallback mode: only val rows, bounded per dataset, early stop
                    if split != task.val_split:
                        stats["rows_by_split"]["train" if split in train_splits else "other"] += 1
                        continue
                    target_split = "val"
                else:
                    if split in train_splits:
                        target_split = "train"
                    elif split == task.val_split:
                        target_split = "val"
                    else:
                        stats["rows_by_split"]["other"] += 1
                        continue
                stats["rows_by_split"][target_split] += 1
                dataset = str(row.get("dataset") or task.default_dataset or source.stem)
                if not row.get("dataset"):
                    row["dataset"] = dataset
                if (task.include is not None and dataset not in task.include) or dataset in task.exclude:
                    stats["rows_excluded_by_dataset"] += 1
                    continue
                if quota is not None:
                    fallback_datasets_seen.add(dataset)
                    if fallback_rows.get(dataset, 0) >= quota:
                        if all(fallback_rows.get(d, 0) >= quota for d in fallback_datasets_seen):
                            break  # every dataset seen in this file has reached its quota
                        continue
                    fallback_rows[dataset] = fallback_rows.get(dataset, 0) + 1
                sample_id = str(row.get("sample_id") or f"{dataset}:{stats['rows_read']}")
                rng = random.Random(f"jev-prepare:{sample_id}")
                try:
                    examples, drops = jev_row_to_examples(row, family=task.family, label_space=label_space, policy=policy, rng=rng)
                except Exception as exc:  # noqa: BLE001 - a bad row must not kill the whole prepare
                    stats["rows_adapter_error"] += 1
                    if warned < 5:
                        warned += 1
                        logger.warning("%s: adapter failed on row %s: %s", source.name, sample_id, exc)
                    continue
                for name, count in (drops or {}).items():
                    stats["drops"][name] = stats["drops"].get(name, 0) + int(count)
                cap = caps[target_split]
                for ex in examples:
                    line = json.dumps(ex.to_dict(), ensure_ascii=False)
                    k = len(ex.options)
                    if cap is None:
                        writer_for(target_split).write(line + "\n")
                        _stats_add_example(stats, target_split, ex.dataset, ex.qtype, k)
                    else:
                        stats["capped_candidates"][target_split] += 1
                        heap = reservoirs.setdefault((target_split, ex.dataset), [])
                        h = _stable_hash(ex.id)
                        item = (-h, ex.id, line, (ex.dataset, ex.qtype, k))
                        if len(heap) < cap:
                            heapq.heappush(heap, item)
                        elif -h > heap[0][0]:  # smaller hash than the current largest -> replace
                            heapq.heapreplace(heap, item)
                if quota is not None and fallback_rows and all(fallback_rows.get(d, 0) >= quota for d in fallback_datasets_seen):
                    break
    finally:
        for fh in writers.values():
            fh.close()

    capped_meta: dict[str, list[tuple[int, str, str, int]]] = {"train": [], "val": []}
    for split in _SPLITS:
        keys = sorted(key for key in reservoirs if key[0] == split)
        if not keys:
            continue
        path = part_dir / f"part-{task.part_index:05d}.{split}.capped.jsonl"
        with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as fout:
            for key in keys:
                for neg_h, _id, line, (dataset, qtype, k) in sorted(reservoirs[key], key=lambda it: (-it[0], it[1])):
                    fout.write(line + "\n")
                    capped_meta[split].append((-neg_h, dataset, qtype, k))
        capped_parts[split] = str(path)
    reservoirs.clear()
    if quota is not None:
        stats["val_fallback_rows_per_dataset"] = dict(sorted(fallback_rows.items()))
    return _FileResult(
        part_index=task.part_index,
        source=str(source),
        uncapped_parts=uncapped_parts,
        capped_parts=capped_parts,
        capped_meta=capped_meta,
        stats=stats,
        seconds=time.time() - t0,
    )


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# Prepare: orchestration
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
@dataclass
class PreparedData:
    """Result of :func:`prepare_data`."""

    train_files: list[Path]
    val_files: list[Path]
    stats: dict[str, Any]
    families: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)
    processed_dir: Path | None = None
    manifest_path: Path | None = None
    #: families whose val.jsonl is in ``val_files`` but whose train.jsonl is NOT in ``train_files``
    val_only_families: list[str] = field(default_factory=list)

    @property
    def n_train(self) -> int:
        return sum(int(s.get("examples", {}).get("train", 0)) for f, s in self.stats.get("families", {}).items()
                   if f not in self.val_only_families)

    @property
    def n_val(self) -> int:
        return sum(int(s.get("examples", {}).get("val", 0)) for s in self.stats.get("families", {}).values())


@dataclass(frozen=True)
class _PrepareSettings:
    """The config subset that determines the processed files (part of the cache key)."""

    root: Path
    mode: str
    families: tuple[str, ...]
    processed_dir: Path
    train_splits: tuple[str, ...]
    val_split: str
    val_fallback_per_dataset: int
    max_val_per_dataset: int | None
    max_train_per_dataset: int | None
    include: frozenset[str] | None
    exclude: frozenset[str]
    over_limit_policy: str
    workers: int
    rebuild: bool
    alphabet_size: int | None
    max_options: int | None
    dedup_val_against_train: bool = True
    #: families prepared for validation only (their train.jsonl is never used)
    val_only_families: tuple[str, ...] = ()
    #: effective source mode of every prepared family: ((family, "sampled"|"full"), ...)
    family_modes: tuple[tuple[str, str], ...] = ()
    #: test files whose states must never appear in train/val (data.exclude_test_overlap); () = off
    test_sources: tuple[Path, ...] = ()

    def mode_for(self, family: str) -> str:
        """Effective source mode of ``family`` (``data.family_modes`` override or the global ``data.mode``)."""
        return dict(self.family_modes).get(family, self.mode)

    @property
    def all_families(self) -> tuple[str, ...]:
        """Training families followed by the val-only families (the order they are prepared in)."""
        return self.families + tuple(f for f in self.val_only_families if f not in self.families)

    @classmethod
    def from_config(cls, cfg: Any, label_space: LabelSpace | None) -> _PrepareSettings:
        families = _cfg_get(cfg, "data.families", ["classification", "ranking", "search"])
        if isinstance(families, str):
            families = [f.strip() for f in families.split(",") if f.strip()]
        val_only = _cfg_get(cfg, "data.val_only_families", []) or []
        if isinstance(val_only, str):
            val_only = [f.strip() for f in val_only.split(",") if f.strip()]
        val_only = [str(f) for f in val_only]
        overlap = sorted(set(val_only) & {str(f) for f in families})
        if overlap:
            raise ValueError(f"data.val_only_families {overlap} must not also be in data.families")
        train_splits = _cfg_get(cfg, "data.train_splits", ["train"])
        if isinstance(train_splits, str):
            train_splits = [train_splits]
        train_splits = tuple(str(s) for s in train_splits if str(s) != _TEST_SPLIT)
        if not train_splits:
            raise ValueError("data.train_splits must contain at least one non-test split")
        val_split = str(_cfg_get(cfg, "data.val_split", "val"))
        if val_split == _TEST_SPLIT:
            raise ValueError("data.val_split may not be 'test'")
        mode = str(_cfg_get(cfg, "data.mode", "sampled")).lower()
        if mode not in ("sampled", "full"):
            raise ValueError(f"data.mode must be 'sampled' or 'full', got {mode!r}")
        overrides = _cfg_get(cfg, "data.family_modes", {}) or {}
        if not isinstance(overrides, Mapping):
            raise ValueError(f"data.family_modes must be a mapping family -> sampled|full, got {overrides!r}")
        fam_modes: list[tuple[str, str]] = []
        for fam in [str(f) for f in families] + val_only:
            fam_mode = str(overrides.get(fam) or mode).lower()
            if fam_mode not in ("sampled", "full"):
                raise ValueError(f"data.family_modes.{fam} must be 'sampled' or 'full', got {fam_mode!r}")
            fam_modes.append((fam, fam_mode))
        max_train = _cfg_get(cfg, "data.max_train_per_dataset")
        max_val = _cfg_get(cfg, "data.max_val_per_dataset")
        labels = getattr(label_space, "labels", None) if label_space is not None else None
        return cls(
            root=_resolve_path(_cfg_get(cfg, "data.root", "data/SearchJev-V1")),
            mode=mode,
            families=tuple(str(f) for f in families),
            processed_dir=_resolve_path(_cfg_get(cfg, "data.processed_dir", "data/processed")),
            train_splits=train_splits,
            val_split=val_split,
            val_fallback_per_dataset=int(_cfg_get(cfg, "data.val_fallback_per_dataset", 300) or 0),
            max_val_per_dataset=None if max_val is None else int(max_val),
            max_train_per_dataset=None if max_train is None else int(max_train),
            include=_as_name_set(_cfg_get(cfg, "data.include_datasets")),
            exclude=_as_name_set(_cfg_get(cfg, "data.exclude_datasets")) or frozenset(),
            over_limit_policy=str(_cfg_get(cfg, "labels.over_limit_policy", "drop")).lower(),
            workers=max(1, int(_cfg_get(cfg, "data.prepare_workers", 8) or 1)),
            rebuild=bool(_cfg_get(cfg, "data.rebuild", False)),
            alphabet_size=None if labels is None else len(labels),
            max_options=_label_space_max_options(label_space),
            dedup_val_against_train=bool(_cfg_get(cfg, "data.dedup_val_against_train", True)),
