                perms.append(p)
                idx.append(i)
            if not idx:
                break
            sub = [examples[i] for i in idx]
            sub_perms = [perms[i] for i in idx]
            logits, _ = self._canonical_logits(sub, sub_perms)
            for i, lg in zip(idx, logits):
                sums[i] = sums[i] + softmax_with_temperature(lg, self._temperature(examples[i], temperature, type_temperature))
                logit_sums[i] = logit_sums[i] + np.asarray(lg, dtype=np.float64)
                counts[i] += 1
        out = []
        for i, ex in enumerate(examples):
            probs = sums[i] / counts[i]
            out.append(self._make_result(ex, (logit_sums[i] / counts[i]).tolist(), temperature, truncated=truncated[i],
                                         type_temperature=type_temperature, probs=probs))
            out[-1].extra["tta_trials"] = counts[i] - 1
        return out

    def decide(
        self,
        state: Any,
        question: str,
        options: Sequence[str] | Mapping[str, Any] | Sequence[Option] | None = None,
        question_type: str = "choice",
        temperature: float | None = None,
    ) -> DecisionResult:
        """Decide a single ad-hoc question.

        Args:
            state: raw state (string, dict, list or JSON string); rendered with
                :func:`training.schema.render_state`.
            question: the instructions text.
            options: see :func:`build_options` (ignored for ``noul``).
            question_type: ``choice`` | ``noul`` | ``score``.
            temperature: override the calibrated temperature.
        """
        qt = question_type.lower()
        if qt not in ("choice", "noul", "score"):
            raise ValueError(f"unknown question_type {question_type!r}")
        opts = build_options(options, qt)
        if len(opts) > self.label_space.max_options:
            raise ValueError(f"{len(opts)} options exceed the label space ({self.label_space.max_options})")
        state_text = render_state(state)
        if not state_text:
            raise ValueError("empty state")
        ex = DecisionExample(
            id="adhoc#q",
            dataset="adhoc",
            family="",
            task="",
            split="",
            qtype=qt,
            state=state_text,
            question=str(question),
            options=opts,
            target_idx=None,
            target_dist=None,
            meta={},
        )
        return self.decide_batch([ex], temperature=temperature)[0]

    def answer_jev(self, row: Mapping[str, Any], family: str = "") -> dict[str, Any]:
        """Answer every question of one JEV API row.

        Returns ``{"answers": {qid: TypeSafe-style answer}, "results": {qid:
        DecisionResult}, "dropped": {reason: n}}``.  Answer formats:

        * ``noul``  -> ``{"type": "noul", "noul": P(true)}``
        * ``choice`` -> ``{"type": "choice", "choice": key, "confidence": c, "probabilities": {key: p}}``
        * ``score`` -> ``{"type": "score", "score": E[level], "confidence": c,
          "legend": {"i": desc}, "probabilities": {"i": p}}``
        """
        row = dict(row)
        examples, drops = jev_row_to_examples(
            row, family=family, label_space=self.label_space, require_answer=False
        )
        results = self.decide_batch(examples) if examples else []
        sample_id = str(row.get("sample_id", ""))
        questions = row.get("questions") or {}
        answers: dict[str, Any] = {}
        by_qid: dict[str, DecisionResult] = {}
        for ex, res in zip(examples, results):
            qid = _qid_of(ex.id, sample_id)
            by_qid[qid] = res
            if ex.qtype == "noul":
                answers[qid] = {"type": "noul", "noul": float(res.probabilities.get("true", 0.0))}
            elif ex.qtype == "choice":
                answers[qid] = {
                    "type": "choice",
                    "choice": res.choice,
                    "confidence": res.confidence,
                    "probabilities": dict(res.probabilities),
                }
            else:  # score
                criteria = (questions.get(qid) or {}).get("criteria")
                if isinstance(criteria, list):
                    legend = {str(i): str(d) for i, d in enumerate(criteria)}
                else:
                    legend = {o.key: o.text for o in ex.options}
                answers[qid] = {
                    "type": "score",
                    "score": float(res.expected_score if res.expected_score is not None else res.choice_index),
                    "confidence": res.confidence,
                    "legend": legend,
                    "probabilities": {str(k): float(v) for k, v in res.probabilities.items()},
                }
        if drops and any(drops.values()):
            logger.debug("answer_jev(%s): dropped questions %s", sample_id, drops)
        return {"answers": answers, "results": by_qid, "dropped": dict(drops)}

    def iter_decide(
        self, examples: Iterable[DecisionExample], chunk: int = 1024
    ) -> Iterable[tuple[DecisionExample, DecisionResult]]:
        """Stream ``(example, result)`` pairs, deciding ``chunk`` examples at a time."""
        buf: list[DecisionExample] = []
        for ex in examples:
            buf.append(ex)
            if len(buf) >= chunk:
                yield from zip(buf, self.decide_batch(buf))
                buf = []
        if buf:
            yield from zip(buf, self.decide_batch(buf))

    def __repr__(self) -> str:  # pragma: no cover
        return (
            f"JEVPredictor(model={self.model_or_run!r}, device={self.device}, "
            f"labels={len(self.label_space.labels)}, calibrated={bool(self.calibration)})"
        )


__all__ = [
    "DecisionResult",
    "JEVPredictor",
    "build_options",
    "locate_run_dir",
    "softmax_with_temperature",
]

================================================
FILE: training/loss.py
================================================
"""JEV loss: masked cross-entropy / KL over the option menu plus a Brier term.

Given restricted menu logits ``z`` (``[B, K]``, one column per displayed
option, invalid slots masked), the model's distribution over options is

    p_j = softmax(z)_j   (over valid slots only)

and the loss for a (possibly soft) target ``t`` is

    L = L_main + lambda * L_Brier
    L_CE    = -sum_j t_j log p_j
    L_KL    = L_CE - H(t) = sum_j t_j log(t_j / p_j)
    L_Brier = sum_j (p_j - t_j)^2

``ce`` and ``kl`` have identical gradients; ``kl`` merely subtracts the target
entropy so that a perfectly matched soft target gives zero loss. The Brier
term is a proper scoring rule that directly rewards calibrated probabilities.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import torch

_LOSS_TYPES = ("ce", "kl")


def masked_log_softmax(menu_logits: torch.Tensor, label_mask: torch.Tensor) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Log-softmax over valid menu slots; masked slots get ``-inf``.

    Args:
        menu_logits: ``[B, K]`` restricted logits.
        label_mask: ``[B, K]`` bool, ``True`` for valid option slots.
    """
    mask = label_mask.bool()
    masked = menu_logits.float().masked_fill(~mask, float("-inf"))
    return torch.log_softmax(masked, dim=-1)


def menu_probs(
    menu_logits: torch.Tensor,
    label_mask: torch.Tensor,
    temperature: float | torch.Tensor = 1.0,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Probabilities over the menu (masked slots are exactly 0), optionally temperature-scaled.

    Args:
        menu_logits: ``[B, K]`` restricted logits.
        label_mask: ``[B, K]`` bool validity mask.
        temperature: scalar ``T`` (or ``[B]`` / ``[B, 1]`` tensor); logits are divided by ``T``.

    Returns:
        ``[B, K]`` float32 probabilities summing to 1 over valid slots.
    """
    logits = menu_logits.float()
    if isinstance(temperature, torch.Tensor):
        t = temperature.to(logits.device, torch.float32)
        if t.dim() == 1:
            t = t.unsqueeze(-1)
        logits = logits / t
    elif float(temperature) != 1.0:
        logits = logits / float(temperature)
    mask = label_mask.bool()
    logp = masked_log_softmax(logits, mask)
    return logp.exp().masked_fill(~mask, 0.0)


def jev_loss(
    menu_logits: torch.Tensor,
    target_dist: torch.Tensor,
    label_mask: torch.Tensor,
    lambda_brier: float = 0.5,
    loss_type: str = "ce",
    weights: torch.Tensor | None = None,
) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, dict[str, torch.Tensor]]:
    """Compute the JEV loss and its components.

    Args:
        menu_logits: ``[B, K]`` restricted logits (display order).
        target_dist: ``[B, K]`` target distribution (one-hot or soft, display order;
            padded slots may hold anything, they are zeroed by ``label_mask``).
        label_mask: ``[B, K]`` bool, ``True`` for valid option slots.
        lambda_brier: weight of the Brier term.
        loss_type: ``"ce"`` or ``"kl"``.
        weights: optional ``[B]`` per-example weights (label reliability); the loss is ``mean(w * l)`` so
            down-weighted examples contribute proportionally less. ``None`` = all ones.

    Returns:
        ``(loss, components)`` where ``loss`` is a scalar tensor and ``components``
        holds detached scalar tensors ``ce``, ``kl``, ``brier``, ``acc`` (argmax
        agreement with the target argmax) and ``loss``.
    """
    if loss_type not in _LOSS_TYPES:
        raise ValueError(f"loss_type must be one of {_LOSS_TYPES}, got {loss_type!r}")
    if menu_logits.dim() != 2:
        raise ValueError(f"menu_logits must be [B, K], got shape {tuple(menu_logits.shape)}")
    if menu_logits.shape != target_dist.shape or menu_logits.shape != label_mask.shape:
        raise ValueError(
            "menu_logits, target_dist and label_mask must share shape [B, K]; got "
            f"{tuple(menu_logits.shape)}, {tuple(target_dist.shape)}, {tuple(label_mask.shape)}"
        )

    mask = label_mask.bool()
    t = target_dist.to(menu_logits.device, torch.float32).masked_fill(~mask, 0.0)

    logp = masked_log_softmax(menu_logits, mask)
    logp_safe = logp.masked_fill(~mask, 0.0)           # avoid 0 * -inf = nan
    p = logp_safe.exp().masked_fill(~mask, 0.0)        # exp(0) = 1 at masked slots -> zero them

    ce = -(t * logp_safe).sum(-1)                       # [B]
    target_entropy = -torch.special.xlogy(t, t).sum(-1)  # 0 log 0 = 0
    kl = ce - target_entropy
    brier = ((p - t) ** 2).sum(-1)

    main = ce if loss_type == "ce" else kl
    if weights is None:
        loss = main.mean() + float(lambda_brier) * brier.mean()
    else:
        w = weights.to(menu_logits.device, torch.float32).reshape(-1)
        if w.shape[0] != main.shape[0]:
            raise ValueError(f"weights must be [B]={main.shape[0]}, got {tuple(weights.shape)}")
        loss = (w * main).mean() + float(lambda_brier) * (w * brier).mean()

    with torch.no_grad():
        acc = (p.argmax(-1) == t.argmax(-1)).float().mean()
    components = {
        "loss": loss.detach(),
        "ce": ce.mean().detach(),
        "kl": kl.mean().detach(),
        "brier": brier.mean().detach(),
        "acc": acc,
    }
    return loss, components


__all__ = ["jev_loss", "masked_log_softmax", "menu_probs"]



================================================
FILE: training/metrics.py
================================================
"""Numpy-only evaluation metrics for JEV decisions.

All functions take plain ``numpy`` arrays. ``summarize`` is the single entry
point used by the trainer's ``compute_metrics`` and the final evaluation. Probabilities
passed to it must be in **canonical** option order (index 0 == ``"true"`` for
``noul``; index ``i`` == level ``i`` for ``score``).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import math
from collections.abc import Sequence

import numpy as np

#: Question-type ids used by the dataset / collator.
QTYPE_IDS: dict[str, int] = {"choice": 0, "noul": 1, "score": 2}
QTYPE_NAMES: dict[int, str] = {v: k for k, v in QTYPE_IDS.items()}

_EPS = 1e-12


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# primitives
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _rankdata(x: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Ranks (1-based) with ties replaced by their average rank."""
    x = np.asarray(x, dtype=np.float64)
    if x.size == 0:
        return np.zeros(0, dtype=np.float64)
    _, inverse, counts = np.unique(x, return_inverse=True, return_counts=True)
    cum = np.cumsum(counts)
    avg_rank = (cum - counts + 1 + cum) / 2.0
    return avg_rank[inverse.reshape(-1)]


def macro_f1(pred: np.ndarray, gold: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Macro-averaged F1 over the classes that appear in ``gold``.

    A class's F1 is ``2TP / (2TP + FP + FN)``; predictions of classes never seen
    in ``gold`` only count as false positives / false negatives of the gold classes.
    """
    pred = np.asarray(pred).reshape(-1)
    gold = np.asarray(gold).reshape(-1)
    if gold.size == 0:
        return float("nan")
    scores = []
    for c in np.unique(gold):
        tp = float(np.sum((pred == c) & (gold == c)))
        fp = float(np.sum((pred == c) & (gold != c)))
        fn = float(np.sum((pred != c) & (gold == c)))
        denom = 2.0 * tp + fp + fn
        scores.append(2.0 * tp / denom if denom > 0 else 0.0)
    return float(np.mean(scores))


def auroc(scores: np.ndarray, labels: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Area under the ROC curve via the Mann-Whitney U statistic (ties averaged).

    Returns ``nan`` when only one class is present.
    """
    scores = np.asarray(scores, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    labels = np.asarray(labels).reshape(-1).astype(bool)
    n_pos = int(labels.sum())
    n_neg = int(labels.size - n_pos)
    if n_pos == 0 or n_neg == 0:
        return float("nan")
    ranks = _rankdata(scores)
    u = ranks[labels].sum() - n_pos * (n_pos + 1) / 2.0
    return float(u / (n_pos * n_neg))


def ece(conf: np.ndarray, correct: np.ndarray, n_bins: int = 15) -> float:
    """Expected calibration error with ``n_bins`` equal-width confidence bins on ``[0, 1]``."""
    conf = np.asarray(conf, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    correct = np.asarray(correct, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    n = conf.size
    if n == 0:
        return float("nan")
    bins = np.minimum((np.clip(conf, 0.0, 1.0) * n_bins).astype(np.int64), n_bins - 1)
    total = 0.0
    for b in range(n_bins):
        sel = bins == b
        m = int(sel.sum())
        if m == 0:
            continue
        total += abs(correct[sel].mean() - conf[sel].mean()) * m / n
    return float(total)


def spearman(a: np.ndarray, b: np.ndarray) -> float:
    """Spearman rank correlation (average ranks for ties); ``nan`` if either input is constant."""
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    b = np.asarray(b, dtype=np.float64).reshape(-1)
    if a.size < 2 or a.size != b.size:
        return float("nan")
    ra = _rankdata(a)
    rb = _rankdata(b)
    ra = ra - ra.mean()
    rb = rb - rb.mean()
    denom = math.sqrt(float((ra * ra).sum()) * float((rb * rb).sum()))
    if denom <= 0.0:
        return float("nan")
    return float((ra * rb).sum() / denom)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# summary
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _finite_or_none(x: float) -> float | None:
    x = float(x)
    return x if math.isfinite(x) else None


def _prepare_probs(probs: np.ndarray, num_options: np.ndarray) -> np.ndarray:
    """Clean a ``[N, Kmax]`` probability matrix: drop padding (negative values and
    slots beyond ``num_options``), then renormalise each row."""
    p = np.asarray(probs, dtype=np.float64)
    if p.ndim == 1:
        p = p[:, None]
    p = np.where(p < 0.0, 0.0, p)
    kmax = p.shape[1]
    valid = np.arange(kmax)[None, :] < np.asarray(num_options).reshape(-1, 1)
    p = np.where(valid, p, 0.0)
    row_sum = p.sum(axis=1, keepdims=True)
    p = np.where(row_sum > 0.0, p / np.maximum(row_sum, _EPS), p)
    return p


def _per_example(probs: np.ndarray, target_idx: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, np.ndarray]:
    """Per-example statistics shared by every block."""
    n, kmax = probs.shape
    rows = np.arange(n)
    gold = np.asarray(target_idx, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
    gold_c = np.clip(gold, 0, kmax - 1)
    p_gold = probs[rows, gold_c]
    onehot = np.zeros_like(probs)
    onehot[rows, gold_c] = 1.0
    pred = probs.argmax(axis=1)
    conf = probs.max(axis=1)
    if kmax >= 2:
        top2 = np.partition(probs, kmax - 2, axis=1)[:, -2:]
        margin = top2[:, 1] - top2[:, 0]
    else:
        margin = conf.copy()
    with np.errstate(divide="ignore", invalid="ignore"):
        entropy = -np.sum(np.where(probs > 0.0, probs * np.log(np.maximum(probs, _EPS)), 0.0), axis=1)
    return {
        "gold": gold,
        "pred": pred,
        "correct": (pred == gold).astype(np.float64),
        "nll": -np.log(np.clip(p_gold, _EPS, None)),
        "brier": np.sum((probs - onehot) ** 2, axis=1),
        "conf": conf,
        "entropy": entropy,
        "margin": margin,
    }


def _block(stats: dict[str, np.ndarray], idx: np.ndarray, n_bins: int) -> dict[str, float | int | None]:
    """Overall-style metrics for the subset ``idx``."""
    n = int(idx.size)
    if n == 0:
        return {"n": 0}
    correct = stats["correct"][idx]
    conf = stats["conf"][idx]
    return {
        "n": n,
        "accuracy": _finite_or_none(correct.mean()),
        "nll": _finite_or_none(stats["nll"][idx].mean()),
        "brier": _finite_or_none(stats["brier"][idx].mean()),
        "ece": _finite_or_none(ece(conf, correct, n_bins)),
        "mean_confidence": _finite_or_none(conf.mean()),
        "mean_entropy": _finite_or_none(stats["entropy"][idx].mean()),
        "mean_margin": _finite_or_none(stats["margin"][idx].mean()),
    }


def _noul_extras(probs: np.ndarray, stats: dict[str, np.ndarray], idx: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, float | None]:
    p_true = probs[idx, 0]
    is_true = stats["gold"][idx] == 0
    return {"auroc": _finite_or_none(auroc(p_true, is_true))}


def _score_extras(probs: np.ndarray, stats: dict[str, np.ndarray], idx: np.ndarray, num_options: np.ndarray) -> dict[str, float | None]:
    sub = probs[idx]
    levels = np.arange(sub.shape[1], dtype=np.float64)[None, :]
    expected = (sub * levels).sum(axis=1)
    gold = stats["gold"][idx].astype(np.float64)
    pred = stats["pred"][idx].astype(np.float64)
    k = np.asarray(num_options)[idx].astype(np.float64)
    within1 = np.abs(pred - gold) <= 1.0
    return {
        "mae": _finite_or_none(np.abs(expected - gold).mean()),
        "mae_argmax": _finite_or_none(np.abs(pred - gold).mean()),
        "exact_acc": _finite_or_none(stats["correct"][idx].mean()),
        "within_1_acc": _finite_or_none(within1.mean()),
        "spearman": _finite_or_none(spearman(expected, gold)),
        "mean_expected_level": _finite_or_none(expected.mean()),
        "mean_levels": _finite_or_none(k.mean()),
    }


def summarize(
    probs: np.ndarray,
    target_idx: np.ndarray,
    qtype_id: np.ndarray,
    num_options: np.ndarray,
    datasets: Sequence[str] | None = None,
    n_bins: int = 15,
) -> dict:
    """Summarise predictions.

    Args:
        probs: ``[N, Kmax]`` probabilities in canonical option order (slots beyond
            ``num_options[i]`` are ignored; negative padding values are ignored).
        target_idx: ``[N]`` canonical gold index.
        qtype_id: ``[N]`` question-type id (``0`` choice, ``1`` noul, ``2`` score).
        num_options: ``[N]`` number of valid options per example.
        datasets: optional ``[N]`` dataset names for a per-dataset breakdown.
        n_bins: bins for ECE.

    Returns:
        ``{"overall": {...}, "per_qtype": {name: {...}}, "per_dataset": {name: {...}}}``.
        ``overall`` holds ``n, accuracy, nll, brier, ece, mean_confidence,
        mean_entropy, mean_margin``; ``noul`` adds ``auroc``; ``score`` adds
        ``mae, mae_argmax, exact_acc, within_1_acc, spearman``; per-dataset blocks
        add ``macro_f1`` and ``qtype`` (plus the type extras when the dataset is
        homogeneous in question type). Non-finite values are reported as ``None``.
    """
    target_idx = np.asarray(target_idx, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
    qtype_id = np.asarray(qtype_id, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
    num_options = np.asarray(num_options, dtype=np.int64).reshape(-1)
    n = int(target_idx.size)
    if n == 0:
        return {"overall": {"n": 0}, "per_qtype": {}, "per_dataset": {}}
    p = _prepare_probs(probs, num_options)
    if p.shape[0] != n or qtype_id.size != n or num_options.size != n:
        raise ValueError(
            f"Inconsistent lengths: probs {p.shape[0]}, target_idx {n}, qtype_id {qtype_id.size}, num_options {num_options.size}"
        )
    if datasets is not None and len(datasets) != n:
        raise ValueError(f"datasets has {len(datasets)} entries, expected {n}")

    stats = _per_example(p, target_idx)
    all_idx = np.arange(n)
    out: dict = {"overall": _block(stats, all_idx, n_bins), "per_qtype": {}, "per_dataset": {}}

    for qid in sorted(set(int(q) for q in qtype_id.tolist())):
        idx = np.nonzero(qtype_id == qid)[0]
        name = QTYPE_NAMES.get(qid, f"qtype{qid}")
        block = _block(stats, idx, n_bins)
        block.update(_type_extras(name, p, stats, idx, num_options))
        out["per_qtype"][name] = block

    if datasets is not None:
        ds = np.asarray([str(d) for d in datasets], dtype=object)
        for name in sorted(set(ds.tolist())):
            idx = np.nonzero(ds == name)[0]
            block = _block(stats, idx, n_bins)
            block["macro_f1"] = _finite_or_none(macro_f1(stats["pred"][idx], stats["gold"][idx]))
            qtypes = sorted(set(int(q) for q in qtype_id[idx].tolist()))
            if len(qtypes) == 1:
                qname = QTYPE_NAMES.get(qtypes[0], f"qtype{qtypes[0]}")
                block["qtype"] = qname
                block.update(_type_extras(qname, p, stats, idx, num_options))
            else:
                block["qtype"] = "mixed"
                block["qtypes"] = [QTYPE_NAMES.get(q, f"qtype{q}") for q in qtypes]
            out["per_dataset"][name] = block
    return out


def _type_extras(name: str, p: np.ndarray, stats: dict[str, np.ndarray], idx: np.ndarray, num_options: np.ndarray) -> dict:
    if idx.size == 0:
        return {}
    if name == "noul":
        return _noul_extras(p, stats, idx)
    if name == "score":
        return _score_extras(p, stats, idx, num_options)
    return {}


def flatten_summary(summary: dict, prefix: str = "") -> dict[str, float]:
    """Flatten a ``summarize`` result into ``{key: number}`` (for Trainer logging).

    ``overall`` keys are emitted bare, per-type keys as ``<qtype>_<key>`` and
    per-dataset keys as ``ds_<dataset>_<key>``. ``None`` / non-numeric values are dropped.
    """
    flat: dict[str, float] = {}

    def _put(key: str, value: object) -> None:
        if isinstance(value, bool) or value is None:
            return
        if isinstance(value, (int, float)) and math.isfinite(float(value)):
            flat[prefix + key] = float(value)

    for k, v in (summary.get("overall") or {}).items():
        _put(k, v)
    for qname, block in (summary.get("per_qtype") or {}).items():
        for k, v in block.items():
            _put(f"{qname}_{k}", v)
    for dname, block in (summary.get("per_dataset") or {}).items():
        for k, v in block.items():
            _put(f"ds_{dname}_{k}", v)
    return flat


__all__ = [
    "QTYPE_IDS",
    "QTYPE_NAMES",
    "macro_f1",
    "auroc",
    "ece",
    "spearman",
    "summarize",
    "flatten_summary",
]



================================================
FILE: training/modeling.py
================================================
"""Model utilities for Qwen3.5-JEV.

This module owns everything that touches the backbone:

* the size table (``0.8B`` .. ``27B`` -> Hugging Face model ids),
* tokenizer loading (left padding, pad token),
* model loading in bf16 (optionally 4-bit NF4 for QLoRA),
* LoRA injection (matched by module leaf name, covering both the
  Gated-DeltaNet ``linear_attn.*`` projections and the full-attention
  ``self_attn.*`` projections),
* the *restricted first-token readout* ``restricted_menu_logits`` which is
  the heart of the JEV adaptation: with left padding and ``logits_to_keep=1``
  we take the logits of the very next token and gather only the label-token
  columns (``A``, ``B``, ...) so that the model becomes a K-way classifier over
  a dynamic option menu without adding any head,
* ``load_for_inference`` for run directories produced by ``start_training.py``.

Nothing here loads weights at import time; the module is importable without a
GPU.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import json
import logging
import os
import re
from collections.abc import Mapping
from pathlib import Path
from typing import Any

import torch

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

#: Model-size table. The ``base`` variant appends ``-Base`` to the id.
SIZES: dict[str, str] = {
    "0.8B": "Qwen/Qwen3.5-0.8B",
    "2B": "Qwen/Qwen3.5-2B",
    "4B": "Qwen/Qwen3.5-4B",
    "9B": "Qwen/Qwen3.5-9B",
    "27B": "Qwen/Qwen3.5-27B",
}

#: Default LoRA targets: full attention, Gated-DeltaNet linear attention, MLP.
DEFAULT_LORA_TARGETS: list[str] = [
    "q_proj", "k_proj", "v_proj", "o_proj",
    "gate_proj", "up_proj", "down_proj",
    "in_proj_qkv", "in_proj_z", "in_proj_b", "in_proj_a", "out_proj",
]

_DTYPES: dict[str, torch.dtype] = {
    "bfloat16": torch.bfloat16,
    "bf16": torch.bfloat16,
    "float16": torch.float16,
    "fp16": torch.float16,
    "half": torch.float16,
    "float32": torch.float32,
    "fp32": torch.float32,
    "float": torch.float32,
}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# small helpers
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def to_torch_dtype(dtype: str | torch.dtype | None) -> torch.dtype:
    """Map a dtype name (``"bfloat16"``, ``"bf16"``, ...) or ``torch.dtype`` to ``torch.dtype``."""
    if dtype is None:
        return torch.bfloat16
    if isinstance(dtype, torch.dtype):
        return dtype
    key = str(dtype).lower().replace("torch.", "")
    if key not in _DTYPES:
        raise ValueError(f"Unknown dtype {dtype!r}; expected one of {sorted(_DTYPES)}")
    return _DTYPES[key]


def normalize_size(size: str) -> str:
    """Normalise a size spelling (``4b``, ``4B``, ``Qwen3.5-4B``, ``qwen/qwen3.5-4b-base``) to a ``SIZES`` key.

    Raises:
        ValueError: if the size cannot be matched.
    """
    s = str(size).strip()
    m = re.search(r"(\d+(?:\.\d+)?)\s*[bB]", s)
    if m is None:
        raise ValueError(f"Cannot parse model size from {size!r}; expected one of {sorted(SIZES)}")
    num = m.group(1)
    if num.endswith(".0"):
        num = num[:-2]
    key = f"{num}B"
    if key not in SIZES:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported model size {size!r}; expected one of {sorted(SIZES)}")
    return key


def resolve_model_id(size: str, variant: str = "instruct") -> str:
    """Return the Hugging Face model id for ``size`` and ``variant`` (``instruct`` | ``base``)."""
    key = normalize_size(size)
    v = str(variant).lower().strip()
    if v in {"instruct", "it", "chat", ""}:
        return SIZES[key]
    if v in {"base", "pretrained", "pt"}:
        return SIZES[key] + "-Base"
    raise ValueError(f"Unknown model variant {variant!r}; expected 'instruct' or 'base'")


def _cfg_get(cfg: Any, key: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
    """Read ``key`` from a mapping or attribute-style config object."""
    if cfg is None:
        return default
    if isinstance(cfg, Mapping):
        return cfg.get(key, default)
    return getattr(cfg, key, default)


def _set_config_flag(config: Any, name: str, value: Any) -> None:
    """Set ``name`` on a model config and on its ``text_config`` when present (multimodal configs)."""
    if config is None:
        return
    if hasattr(config, name):
        setattr(config, name, value)
    text_config = getattr(config, "text_config", None)
    if text_config is not None and hasattr(text_config, name):
        setattr(text_config, name, value)


def _local_rank_device() -> str:
    """Device string for this process: ``cuda:<LOCAL_RANK>`` when CUDA is available, else ``cpu``."""
    if torch.cuda.is_available():
        return f"cuda:{int(os.environ.get('LOCAL_RANK', 0))}"
    return "cpu"


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# tokenizer / model loading
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def load_tokenizer(name_or_path: str) -> Any:
    """Load the tokenizer with **left** padding and a pad token.

    Left padding is mandatory for the restricted readout: the answer position
    must be the last column of the batch for every row.
    """
    from transformers import AutoTokenizer

    tokenizer = AutoTokenizer.from_pretrained(str(name_or_path))
    tokenizer.padding_side = "left"
    if tokenizer.pad_token is None:
        if tokenizer.eos_token is None:
            raise ValueError("Tokenizer has neither pad_token nor eos_token; cannot pad batches")
        tokenizer.pad_token = tokenizer.eos_token
        logger.info("pad_token missing; using eos_token %r as pad", tokenizer.eos_token)
    logger.info(
        "Loaded tokenizer %s (%s): vocab=%d pad=%r(%d) padding_side=%s chat_template=%s",
        name_or_path, type(tokenizer).__name__, len(tokenizer), tokenizer.pad_token,
        tokenizer.pad_token_id, tokenizer.padding_side, bool(getattr(tokenizer, "chat_template", None)),
    )
    return tokenizer


def load_model(
    name_or_path: str,
    *,
    dtype: str | torch.dtype = "bfloat16",
    attn_implementation: str = "sdpa",
    load_in_4bit: bool = False,
    gradient_checkpointing: bool = True,
    device_map: Any | None = None,
) -> Any:
    """Load a causal LM backbone (``Qwen3_5ForCausalLM`` for Qwen3.5 checkpoints).

    Args:
        name_or_path: HF id or local directory.
        dtype: compute / weight dtype (``bfloat16`` by default; transformers 5.x ``dtype`` kwarg).
        attn_implementation: ``sdpa`` (default) or ``eager``.
        load_in_4bit: QLoRA loading with bitsandbytes NF4 + double quantisation and
            bf16 compute; ``peft.prepare_model_for_kbit_training`` is applied.
        gradient_checkpointing: enable non-reentrant activation checkpointing and
            disable the KV cache.
        device_map: passed through to ``from_pretrained``. ``None`` leaves the
            weights on CPU (the Trainer moves them), except for 4-bit loading where
            the current local-rank GPU is used because bitsandbytes needs CUDA.

    Returns:
        The loaded model (a ``PreTrainedModel``).
    """
    from transformers import AutoModelForCausalLM

    torch_dtype = to_torch_dtype(dtype)
    kwargs: dict[str, Any] = {
        "dtype": torch_dtype,
        "attn_implementation": attn_implementation,
    }
    if load_in_4bit:
        from transformers import BitsAndBytesConfig

        kwargs["quantization_config"] = BitsAndBytesConfig(
            load_in_4bit=True,
            bnb_4bit_quant_type="nf4",
            bnb_4bit_compute_dtype=torch_dtype,
            bnb_4bit_use_double_quant=True,
        )
        if device_map is None:
            device_map = {"": _local_rank_device()}
    if device_map is not None:
        kwargs["device_map"] = device_map

    logger.info(
        "Loading model %s (dtype=%s attn=%s 4bit=%s device_map=%s)",
        name_or_path, torch_dtype, attn_implementation, load_in_4bit, device_map,
    )
    model = AutoModelForCausalLM.from_pretrained(str(name_or_path), **kwargs)
    logger.info("Loaded %s", type(model).__name__)

    gc_kwargs = {"use_reentrant": False}
    if load_in_4bit:
        from peft import prepare_model_for_kbit_training

        model = prepare_model_for_kbit_training(
            model,
            use_gradient_checkpointing=gradient_checkpointing,
            gradient_checkpointing_kwargs=gc_kwargs,
        )
    elif gradient_checkpointing:
        model.gradient_checkpointing_enable(gradient_checkpointing_kwargs=gc_kwargs)

    if gradient_checkpointing:
        _set_config_flag(model.config, "use_cache", False)
        logger.info("Gradient checkpointing enabled (non-reentrant); use_cache=False")

    stats = count_parameters(model)
    logger.info("Parameters: total=%.3fB", stats["total"] / 1e9)
    return model


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# LoRA
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def linear_leaf_names(model: torch.nn.Module) -> set[str]:
    """Leaf names of all linear-like modules (``nn.Linear`` and bitsandbytes ``Linear4bit``/``Linear8bitLt``)."""
    names: set[str] = set()
    for name, module in model.named_modules():
        cls = type(module).__name__
        if isinstance(module, torch.nn.Linear) or cls in {"Linear4bit", "Linear8bitLt"}:
            names.add(name.rsplit(".", 1)[-1])
    return names


def resolve_lora_targets(model: torch.nn.Module, targets: list[str] | str | None) -> tuple[list[str], list[str]]:
    """Split requested LoRA targets into ``(present, missing)`` by matching module leaf names."""
    if targets is None:
        targets = list(DEFAULT_LORA_TARGETS)
    if isinstance(targets, str):
        targets = [t.strip() for t in targets.split(",") if t.strip()]
    leafs = linear_leaf_names(model)
    present = [t for t in targets if t in leafs]
    missing = [t for t in targets if t not in leafs]
    return present, missing


def apply_lora(model: torch.nn.Module, lora_cfg: Any) -> Any:
    """Wrap ``model`` in a PEFT LoRA adapter.

    Args:
        model: backbone from :func:`load_model`.
        lora_cfg: mapping or attribute object with ``r``, ``alpha``, ``dropout``,
            ``target_modules`` (list of module leaf names, e.g. ``q_proj``,
            ``in_proj_qkv``); missing keys fall back to ``r=16, alpha=32,
            dropout=0.05`` and :data:`DEFAULT_LORA_TARGETS`.

    Returns:
        A ``peft.PeftModel`` with ``task_type="CAUSAL_LM"``.
    """
    from peft import LoraConfig, get_peft_model

    r = int(_cfg_get(lora_cfg, "r", 16))
    alpha = int(_cfg_get(lora_cfg, "alpha", _cfg_get(lora_cfg, "lora_alpha", 2 * r)))
    dropout = float(_cfg_get(lora_cfg, "dropout", _cfg_get(lora_cfg, "lora_dropout", 0.05)))
    requested = _cfg_get(lora_cfg, "target_modules", None)
    present, missing = resolve_lora_targets(model, requested)
    if missing:
        logger.warning("LoRA targets not found in model and skipped: %s", missing)
    if not present:
        raise ValueError(
            f"None of the LoRA target modules {requested!r} exist in the model; "
            f"available linear leaf names: {sorted(linear_leaf_names(model))}"
        )

    if getattr(model, "is_gradient_checkpointing", False):
        # Required so that activations flowing into checkpointed blocks carry
        # grad even though the (frozen) embedding output does not.
        if hasattr(model, "enable_input_require_grads"):
            model.enable_input_require_grads()

    config = LoraConfig(
        task_type="CAUSAL_LM",
        r=r,
        lora_alpha=alpha,
        lora_dropout=dropout,
        bias="none",
        target_modules=list(present),
    )
    peft_model = get_peft_model(model, config)
    stats = count_parameters(peft_model)
    logger.info(
        "LoRA applied: r=%d alpha=%d dropout=%.3f targets=%s | trainable=%.2fM / total=%.2fM (%.3f%%)",
        r, alpha, dropout, present, stats["trainable"] / 1e6, stats["total"] / 1e6, stats["trainable_percent"],
    )
    return peft_model


def load_lora_for_training(model: torch.nn.Module, adapter_path: str) -> Any:
    """Load an existing LoRA adapter as a *trainable* initialisation (stage-2 training).

    Args:
        model: backbone from :func:`load_model` (same base as the adapter).
        adapter_path: directory produced by a previous run (``<run>/adapter``).

    Returns:
        A trainable ``peft.PeftModel`` whose LoRA weights start from ``adapter_path``.
    """
    from peft import PeftModel

    if getattr(model, "is_gradient_checkpointing", False) and hasattr(model, "enable_input_require_grads"):
        model.enable_input_require_grads()
    peft_model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(model, adapter_path, is_trainable=True)
    stats = count_parameters(peft_model)
    logger.info(
        "LoRA adapter loaded for continued training from %s | trainable=%.2fM / total=%.2fM (%.3f%%)",
        adapter_path, stats["trainable"] / 1e6, stats["total"] / 1e6, stats["trainable_percent"],
    )
    return peft_model


def count_parameters(model: torch.nn.Module) -> dict[str, int | float]:
    """Return ``{"total", "trainable", "frozen", "trainable_percent"}`` parameter counts."""
    total = 0
    trainable = 0
    for p in model.parameters():
        n = p.numel()
        # bitsandbytes 4-bit params store packed values; report unpacked count when known.
        if type(p).__name__ == "Params4bit" and hasattr(p, "quant_storage"):
            n *= 2
        total += n
        if p.requires_grad:
            trainable += n
    pct = 100.0 * trainable / total if total else 0.0
    return {"total": total, "trainable": trainable, "frozen": total - trainable, "trainable_percent": pct}


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# restricted readout
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def restricted_menu_logits(
    model: torch.nn.Module,
    input_ids: torch.Tensor,
    attention_mask: torch.Tensor,
    label_token_ids: torch.Tensor,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    """Restricted first-token readout.

    Runs the backbone on a **left-padded** batch, keeps only the logits of the
    last position (``logits_to_keep=1``) and gathers the columns of the label
    tokens, giving ``z[b, j] = logit(label_j | prompt_b)``.

    Args:
        model: backbone or PEFT model (may be DDP-wrapped).
        input_ids: ``[B, T]`` left-padded token ids.
        attention_mask: ``[B, T]``; its last column must be all ones.
        label_token_ids: ``[B, K]`` token ids of the displayed labels (padded
            slots may hold any valid id; the caller masks them in the loss).

    Returns:
        ``[B, K]`` float32 logits over the menu.
    """
    if attention_mask is not None and attention_mask.shape[-1] > 0:
        if not bool(attention_mask[:, -1].all()):
            raise ValueError(
                "restricted_menu_logits requires LEFT padding: the last attention_mask column must be all ones"
            )
    out = model(
        input_ids=input_ids,
        attention_mask=attention_mask,
        logits_to_keep=1,
        use_cache=False,
    )
    last = out.logits[:, -1, :].float()  # [B, V]
    ids = label_token_ids.to(device=last.device, dtype=torch.long)
    return last.gather(1, ids)


# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# inference loading
# --------------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def _read_yaml(path: Path) -> dict:
    import yaml

    with path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
        data = yaml.safe_load(fh)
    return data if isinstance(data, dict) else {}


def _base_from_resolved_config(run_dir: Path) -> str | None:
    """Recover the base model id from ``<run>/config.resolved.yaml`` if present."""
    cfg_path = run_dir / "config.resolved.yaml"
    if not cfg_path.exists():
        return None
    try:
        cfg = _read_yaml(cfg_path)
    except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive
        logger.warning("Could not parse %s: %s", cfg_path, exc)
        return None
    model_cfg = cfg.get("model", {}) or {}
    if model_cfg.get("name"):
        return str(model_cfg["name"])
    size = model_cfg.get("size")
    if size:
        try:
            return resolve_model_id(str(size), str(model_cfg.get("variant", "instruct")))
        except ValueError:
            return None
    return None


def resolve_run_layout(run_dir_or_name: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
    """Inspect a path / HF id and describe how to load it.

    Returns a dict with keys ``kind`` (``"adapter"`` | ``"local_model"`` | ``"hub"``),
    ``run_dir`` (directory holding ``config.resolved.yaml`` / ``calibration.json`` or
    ``None``), ``weights`` (adapter dir, model dir or HF id) and ``adapter_base``
    (from ``adapter_config.json`` when applicable).
    """
    p = Path(str(run_dir_or_name)).expanduser()
    layout: dict[str, Any] = {"kind": "hub", "run_dir": None, "weights": str(run_dir_or_name), "adapter_base": None}
    if not p.exists():
        return layout
    candidates_adapter = [p, p / "adapter"]
    for cand in candidates_adapter:
        if (cand / "adapter_config.json").exists():
            layout["kind"] = "adapter"
            layout["weights"] = str(cand)
            layout["run_dir"] = str(p if cand == p / "adapter" else p.parent)
            try:
                with (cand / "adapter_config.json").open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                    layout["adapter_base"] = json.load(fh).get("base_model_name_or_path")
            except Exception as exc:  # pragma: no cover - defensive
                logger.warning("Could not read adapter_config.json in %s: %s", cand, exc)
            return layout
    for cand, run_dir in [(p / "merged", p), (p / "model", p), (p, None)]:
        if (cand / "config.json").exists():
            layout["kind"] = "local_model"
            layout["weights"] = str(cand)
            if run_dir is None and (p.parent / "config.resolved.yaml").exists():
                run_dir = p.parent  # e.g. <run>/merged passed directly
            layout["run_dir"] = str(run_dir) if run_dir is not None else None
            return layout
    # A run dir that exists but has no weights yet.
    layout["run_dir"] = str(p) if (p / "config.resolved.yaml").exists() else None
    return layout


def load_for_inference(
    run_dir_or_name: str,
    *,
    base_model: str | None = None,
    dtype: str | torch.dtype = "bfloat16",
    device: str = "cuda",
    merge: bool = False,
    attn_implementation: str = "sdpa",
    load_in_4bit: bool = False,
) -> tuple[Any, Any, dict | None]:
    """Load a trained run (or a plain model id) for inference.

    Accepted inputs:

    * a run directory produced by ``start_training.py`` (``<run>/adapter`` or
      ``<run>/model``; reads ``<run>/config.resolved.yaml`` and
      ``<run>/calibration.json``),
    * an adapter directory itself (``adapter_config.json`` inside),
    * a local model directory or a Hugging Face id (zero-shot base model).

    The base model for an adapter is resolved from ``base_model``, then from
    ``adapter_config.json``, then from ``config.resolved.yaml``.

    Returns:
        ``(model.eval(), tokenizer, calibration_dict_or_None)``.
    """
    layout = resolve_run_layout(run_dir_or_name)
    run_dir = Path(layout["run_dir"]) if layout["run_dir"] else None
    device_map = {"": device} if device else None

    if layout["kind"] == "adapter":
        from peft import PeftModel

        adapter_dir = Path(layout["weights"])
        base = base_model or layout["adapter_base"] or (_base_from_resolved_config(run_dir) if run_dir else None)
        if not base:
            raise ValueError(
                f"Cannot determine the base model for adapter {adapter_dir}; pass base_model=..."
            )
        tok_src = adapter_dir if (adapter_dir / "tokenizer_config.json").exists() else base
        tokenizer = load_tokenizer(str(tok_src))
        base_m = load_model(
            base,
            dtype=dtype,
            attn_implementation=attn_implementation,
            load_in_4bit=load_in_4bit,
            gradient_checkpointing=False,
            device_map=device_map,
        )
        model = PeftModel.from_pretrained(base_m, str(adapter_dir), is_trainable=False)
        logger.info("Loaded LoRA adapter %s on base %s", adapter_dir, base)
        if merge:
            model = model.merge_and_unload()
            logger.info("Merged LoRA weights into the base model")
    else:
        weights = layout["weights"]
        tokenizer = load_tokenizer(str(weights))
        model = load_model(
            weights,
            dtype=dtype,
            attn_implementation=attn_implementation,
            load_in_4bit=load_in_4bit,
            gradient_checkpointing=False,
            device_map=device_map,
        )
        if merge:
            logger.info("merge=True ignored: %s has no adapter", weights)

    _set_config_flag(model.config if hasattr(model, "config") else None, "use_cache", False)
    model.eval()

    calibration: dict | None = None
    if run_dir is not None:
        calib_path = run_dir / "calibration.json"
        if calib_path.exists():
            with calib_path.open("r", encoding="utf-8") as fh:
                calibration = json.load(fh)
            logger.info("Loaded calibration from %s", calib_path)
    return model, tokenizer, calibration


__all__ = [
    "SIZES",
    "DEFAULT_LORA_TARGETS",
    "to_torch_dtype",
    "normalize_size",
    "resolve_model_id",
    "load_tokenizer",
    "load_model",
    "linear_leaf_names",
    "resolve_lora_targets",
    "apply_lora",
    "count_parameters",
    "restricted_menu_logits",
    "resolve_run_layout",
    "load_for_inference",
]

"""``JEVTrainer``: a ``transformers.Trainer`` that trains the restricted first-token readout.

The trainer never passes the JEV-specific batch fields (``label_token_ids``,
``label_mask``, ``target_dist``, ``target_idx``, ``qtype_id``, ``num_options``,
``perm``, ``example_id``) to the backbone. ``compute_loss`` runs the restricted
readout (:func:`training.modeling.restricted_menu_logits`) and
:func:`training.loss.jev_loss`; ``prediction_step`` returns menu probabilities
mapped back to **canonical** option order (using the batch ``perm`` tensor) so
that :func:`training.metrics.summarize` sees canonical indices.

Also provided: :func:`build_training_arguments` (transformers 5.x:
``warmup_steps`` computed from ``warmup_ratio``, ``eval_strategy``) and
:func:`compute_metrics_fn`.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import logging
import math
import os
from collections.abc import Callable, Mapping
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any

import numpy as np
import torch
from transformers import Trainer, TrainingArguments
from transformers.trainer_utils import EvalPrediction

from .loss import jev_loss, menu_probs
from .metrics import flatten_summary, summarize
from .modeling import restricted_menu_logits

if TYPE_CHECKING:  # pragma: no cover
    from .labels import LabelSpace

logger = logging.getLogger(__name__)

#: Keys that are forwarded to the backbone; everything else is a JEV extra.
MODEL_INPUT_KEYS: tuple[str, ...] = ("input_ids", "attention_mask", "position_ids")

#: JEV batch fields produced by the collator (documentation; anything not in
#: ``MODEL_INPUT_KEYS`` is treated as an extra).
JEV_EXTRA_KEYS: tuple[str, ...] = (
    "label_token_ids", "label_mask", "target_dist", "target_idx", "qtype_id", "num_options", "perm", "example_id",
    "weight",
)

_RUNNING_KEYS: tuple[str, ...] = ("ce", "kl", "brier", "acc")


def _cfg_get(cfg: Any, dotted: str, default: Any = None) -> Any:
    """Read ``a.b.c`` from a nested mapping / attribute-style config."""
    cur = cfg
    for part in dotted.split("."):
        if cur is None:
            return default
        if isinstance(cur, Mapping):
            cur = cur.get(part, None)
        else:
            cur = getattr(cur, part, None)
    return default if cur is None else cur


def _pad_perm(perm: Any, batch_size: int, width: int, device: torch.device) -> torch.Tensor | None:
    """Normalise ``perm`` (tensor ``[B, K]`` padded with -1, or list of lists) to a long tensor."""
    if perm is None:
        return None
    if isinstance(perm, torch.Tensor):
        return perm.to(device=device, dtype=torch.long)
    out = torch.full((batch_size, width), -1, dtype=torch.long)
    for b, row in enumerate(perm):
        row_t = torch.as_tensor(list(row), dtype=torch.long)
        out[b, : row_t.numel()] = row_t
    return out.to(device)


class JEVTrainer(Trainer):
    """Trainer for JEV restricted-readout fine-tuning.

    Args:
        label_space: the :class:`training.labels.LabelSpace` (needed for ``max_options``,
            the fixed width of the probability matrix returned by ``prediction_step``).
        lambda_brier: weight of the Brier term (see :func:`training.loss.jev_loss`).
        loss_type: ``"ce"`` or ``"kl"``.

    ``args`` must have ``remove_unused_columns=False`` (the collator emits
    non-model fields) and ``label_names=["target_idx"]``.
    """

    def __init__(
        self,
        *args: Any,
        label_space: LabelSpace,
        lambda_brier: float = 0.5,
        loss_type: str = "ce",
        group_by_length: bool = False,
        max_length: int | None = None,
        **kwargs: Any,
    ) -> None:
        self.group_by_length = bool(group_by_length)
        self.max_prompt_length = max_length
        super().__init__(*args, **kwargs)
        self.label_space = label_space
        self.lambda_brier = float(lambda_brier)
        self.loss_type = str(loss_type)
        # We normalise the loss ourselves (mean over the batch); Trainer then divides
        # by the gradient-accumulation steps, which is the correct behaviour.
        self.model_accepts_loss_kwargs = False
        self._jev_running: dict[str, torch.Tensor | float] = {}
        self._reset_running()
        if self.args.remove_unused_columns:
            raise ValueError("JEVTrainer requires TrainingArguments(remove_unused_columns=False)")
        if not self.args.label_names or "target_idx" not in self.args.label_names:
            logger.warning(
                "TrainingArguments.label_names should contain 'target_idx' (got %r)", self.args.label_names
            )

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # helpers
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    @property
    def menu_width(self) -> int:
        """Fixed width of the probability matrix returned by ``prediction_step``."""
        return int(getattr(self.label_space, "max_options", 0) or 0)

    @staticmethod
    def split_inputs(inputs: Mapping[str, Any]) -> tuple[dict[str, Any], dict[str, Any]]:
        """Split a collated batch into ``(model_inputs, jev_extras)``."""
        model_inputs = {k: v for k, v in inputs.items() if k in MODEL_INPUT_KEYS}
        extras = {k: v for k, v in inputs.items() if k not in MODEL_INPUT_KEYS}
        for required in ("label_token_ids", "label_mask", "target_dist"):
            if required not in extras:
                raise KeyError(f"Batch is missing JEV field {required!r}; got keys {sorted(inputs)}")
        return model_inputs, extras

    def _reset_running(self) -> None:
        self._jev_running = {k: 0.0 for k in _RUNNING_KEYS}
        self._jev_running["count"] = 0.0

    def _accumulate(self, comps: Mapping[str, torch.Tensor], batch_size: int) -> None:
        run = self._jev_running
        for k in _RUNNING_KEYS:
            if k in comps:
                run[k] = run[k] + comps[k].detach().float() * batch_size
        run["count"] = run["count"] + float(batch_size)

    def _menu_forward(
        self, model: torch.nn.Module, inputs: Mapping[str, Any]
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor, torch.Tensor, dict[str, torch.Tensor], dict[str, Any]]:
        """Run the restricted readout and the loss; returns ``(loss, menu_logits, comps, extras)``."""
        model_inputs, extras = self.split_inputs(inputs)
        menu = restricted_menu_logits(
            model, model_inputs["input_ids"], model_inputs.get("attention_mask"), extras["label_token_ids"]
        )
        loss, comps = jev_loss(
            menu, extras["target_dist"], extras["label_mask"], lambda_brier=self.lambda_brier, loss_type=self.loss_type,
            weights=extras.get("weight") if model.training else None,
        )
        return loss, menu, comps, extras

    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    # Trainer overrides
    # ------------------------------------------------------------------ #
    def _get_train_sampler(self, train_dataset: Any = None) -> Any:
        """Length-grouped sampling (``training.group_by_length``) with cached length estimates.

        Consecutive ``per_device_batch x grad_accum`` indices have similar prompt lengths (mega-batches of 50 such
        groups are shuffled, then sorted), so far less padding is computed. Falls back to the default sampler.
        """
        ds = train_dataset if train_dataset is not None else self.train_dataset
        if self.group_by_length and ds is not None:
            from transformers.trainer_pt_utils import LengthGroupedSampler

            from .data import dataset_lengths

            lengths = dataset_lengths(ds, self.max_prompt_length)
            if lengths is not None and len(lengths) == len(ds):
                logger.info("length-grouped sampling over %d items (median est. %d tokens)", len(lengths),
                            sorted(lengths)[len(lengths) // 2] if lengths else 0)
                return LengthGroupedSampler(self.args.train_batch_size * self.args.gradient_accumulation_steps,
                                            lengths=lengths)
            logger.warning("group_by_length: dataset has no length index; using the default sampler")
        return super()._get_train_sampler(train_dataset) if train_dataset is not None else super()._get_train_sampler()
    def compute_loss(
        self,
        model: torch.nn.Module,
        inputs: dict[str, Any],
        return_outputs: bool = False,
        num_items_in_batch: torch.Tensor | int | None = None,
    ) -> torch.Tensor | tuple[torch.Tensor, Any]:
        """Restricted-readout loss for one batch (JEV fields are never passed to the model)."""
        loss, menu, comps, _ = self._menu_forward(model, inputs)
        if model.training:
            self._accumulate(comps, int(menu.shape[0]))
        if return_outputs:
            return loss, {"menu_logits": menu}
        return loss

    def log(self, logs: dict[str, float], start_time: float | None = None) -> None:
        """Inject running averages of the loss components into training logs."""
        count = float(self._jev_running.get("count", 0.0))
        is_train_log = "loss" in logs and not any(k.startswith("eval_") for k in logs)
        if is_train_log and count > 0:
            for k in _RUNNING_KEYS:
                val = self._jev_running.get(k, 0.0)
                val_f = float(val.item()) if isinstance(val, torch.Tensor) else float(val)
                logs[f"jev/{k}"] = round(val_f / count, 6)
            self._reset_running()
        super().log(logs, start_time)

    def prediction_step(
        self,
        model: torch.nn.Module,
        inputs: dict[str, Any],
        prediction_loss_only: bool,
        ignore_keys: list[str] | None = None,
    ) -> tuple[torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None, torch.Tensor | None]:
        """Evaluate one batch.

        Returns ``(loss, probs, labels)`` where ``probs`` is ``[B, max_options]``
        in **canonical** option order (un-permuted with the batch ``perm``, zero
        padded) and ``labels`` is ``[B, 3] = (canonical target_idx, qtype_id, num_options)``.
        """
        inputs = self._prepare_inputs(inputs)
        with torch.no_grad(), self.compute_loss_context_manager():
            loss, menu, _comps, extras = self._menu_forward(model, inputs)
            loss = loss.detach()
            if prediction_loss_only:
                return loss, None, None
            label_mask = extras["label_mask"].bool()
            probs = menu_probs(menu, label_mask)  # [B, K] display order, 0 at masked slots
            bsz, k = probs.shape
            device = probs.device

            target_idx = extras.get("target_idx")
            if target_idx is None:
                target_idx = extras["target_dist"].argmax(-1)
            target_idx = target_idx.to(device=device, dtype=torch.long)
            qtype_id = extras.get("qtype_id")
            if qtype_id is None:
                qtype_id = torch.zeros(bsz, dtype=torch.long, device=device)
            num_options = extras.get("num_options")
            if num_options is None:
                num_options = label_mask.sum(-1)
            num_options = num_options.to(device=device, dtype=torch.long)

            perm = _pad_perm(extras.get("perm"), bsz, k, device)
            width = max(self.menu_width, k)
            canon = torch.zeros(bsz, width, dtype=torch.float32, device=device)
            if perm is None:
                canon[:, :k] = probs
                target_canon = target_idx
            else:
                valid = perm >= 0
                idx = perm.clamp(min=0, max=width - 1)
                canon.scatter_add_(1, idx, probs.masked_fill(~valid, 0.0))
                # an identity for slots the perm does not cover (defensive; perm covers all K)
                uncovered = valid.sum(-1) == 0
                if bool(uncovered.any()):
                    canon[uncovered, :k] = probs[uncovered]
                gathered = perm.gather(1, target_idx.clamp(min=0, max=k - 1).unsqueeze(1)).squeeze(1)
                target_canon = torch.where(gathered >= 0, gathered, target_idx)
            labels_out = torch.stack([target_canon, qtype_id.to(device), num_options], dim=1)
        return loss, canon, labels_out


# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
# TrainingArguments / metrics factories
# ---------------------------------------------------------------------- #
def compute_training_schedule(
    n_train_examples: int,
    per_device_batch_size: int,
    gradient_accumulation_steps: int,
    world_size: int,
    epochs: float,
    max_steps: int = -1,
    warmup_ratio: float = 0.05,
) -> dict[str, int]:
    """Compute ``effective_batch_size``, ``steps_per_epoch``, ``total_steps`` and ``warmup_steps``."""
    world_size = max(int(world_size), 1)
    effective = max(int(per_device_batch_size), 1) * max(int(gradient_accumulation_steps), 1) * world_size
    steps_per_epoch = max(1, math.ceil(max(int(n_train_examples), 0) / effective)) if n_train_examples else 0
    if max_steps is not None and int(max_steps) > 0:
        total = int(max_steps)
    else:
        total = int(math.ceil(steps_per_epoch * float(epochs)))
    warmup = int(round(max(float(warmup_ratio), 0.0) * total)) if total > 0 else 0
    return {
        "effective_batch_size": effective,
        "steps_per_epoch": steps_per_epoch,
        "total_steps": total,
        "warmup_steps": warmup,
    }


def build_training_arguments(
    cfg: Any,
    output_dir: str,
    n_train_examples: int,
    world_size: int,
    *,
    has_eval: bool = True,
) -> TrainingArguments:
    """Build ``TrainingArguments`` from the ``training``/``run`` sections of ``cfg``.

    * ``warmup_steps`` is computed from ``training.warmup_ratio`` (transformers 5.x
      has no ``warmup_ratio``), using ``n_train_examples`` and ``world_size``.
    * gradient checkpointing is handled on the model (``args.gradient_checkpointing=False``).
    * ``remove_unused_columns=False``, ``label_names=["target_idx"]``,
      ``ddp_find_unused_parameters=False``, ``logging_first_step=True``, ``eval_on_start=False``.
    * ``save_only_model`` is left at its default so optimizer state is kept for resuming.

    Args:
        cfg: nested config (mapping or attribute style).
        output_dir: run directory (checkpoints go to ``<output_dir>/checkpoints``).
        n_train_examples: length of the training dataset (across all ranks).
        world_size: number of data-parallel processes.
        has_eval: ``False`` disables in-training evaluation (no validation set).
    """
    t = lambda key, default=None: _cfg_get(cfg, f"training.{key}", default)  # noqa: E731

    epochs = float(t("epochs", 1))
    max_steps = int(t("max_steps", -1) or -1)
    bs = int(t("per_device_train_batch_size", 8))
    ga = int(t("gradient_accumulation_steps", 4))
    schedule = compute_training_schedule(
        n_train_examples, bs, ga, world_size, epochs, max_steps, float(t("warmup_ratio", 0.05))
    )
    logger.info(
        "Schedule: n_train=%d world_size=%d effective_batch=%d steps/epoch=%d total_steps=%d warmup_steps=%d",
        n_train_examples, world_size, schedule["effective_batch_size"], schedule["steps_per_epoch"],
        schedule["total_steps"], schedule["warmup_steps"],
    )

    report_to = _cfg_get(cfg, "run.report_to", "none") or "none"
    if isinstance(report_to, str):
        report_list = [r.strip() for r in report_to.split(",") if r.strip()] or ["none"]
    else:
        report_list = [str(r) for r in report_to] or ["none"]
    run_name = str(_cfg_get(cfg, "run.name", os.path.basename(os.path.normpath(output_dir))))
    if "wandb" in report_list:
        # project / entity (team or user); the run name is run.name via TrainingArguments.run_name.
        # An already exported WANDB_PROJECT / WANDB_ENTITY in the environment wins.
        os.environ.setdefault("WANDB_PROJECT", str(_cfg_get(cfg, "run.wandb_project", "JEV")))
        entity = _cfg_get(cfg, "run.wandb_entity", None)
        if entity:
            os.environ.setdefault("WANDB_ENTITY", str(entity))

    eval_strategy = str(t("eval_strategy", "steps")) if has_eval else "no"
    eval_steps = int(t("eval_steps", 500))
    save_steps = int(t("save_steps", 500))
    seed = int(_cfg_get(cfg, "run.seed", 42))

    args = TrainingArguments(
        output_dir=os.path.join(output_dir, "checkpoints"),
        run_name=run_name,
        num_train_epochs=epochs,
        max_steps=max_steps,
        per_device_train_batch_size=bs,
        per_device_eval_batch_size=int(t("per_device_eval_batch_size", 16)),
        gradient_accumulation_steps=ga,
        learning_rate=float(t("learning_rate", 5e-5)),
        weight_decay=float(t("weight_decay", 0.01)),
        warmup_steps=schedule["warmup_steps"],
        lr_scheduler_type=str(t("lr_scheduler_type", "cosine")),
        max_grad_norm=float(t("max_grad_norm", 1.0)),
        bf16=bool(t("bf16", True)),
        logging_steps=int(t("logging_steps", 10)),
        logging_first_step=True,
        eval_strategy=eval_strategy,
        eval_steps=eval_steps if eval_strategy == "steps" else None,
        save_strategy="steps",
        save_steps=save_steps,
        save_total_limit=int(t("save_total_limit", 3)) if t("save_total_limit", 3) is not None else None,
        seed=seed,
        data_seed=seed,
        dataloader_num_workers=int(t("dataloader_num_workers", 4)),
        dataloader_pin_memory=True,
        optim=str(t("optim", "adamw_torch_fused")),
        report_to=report_list,
        remove_unused_columns=False,
        label_names=["target_idx"],
        gradient_checkpointing=False,
        ddp_find_unused_parameters=False,
        eval_on_start=False,
        disable_tqdm=bool(t("disable_tqdm", False)),
    )
    return args


def compute_metrics_fn(label_space: LabelSpace | None = None) -> Callable[[EvalPrediction], dict[str, float]]:
    """Return a ``compute_metrics`` callable for :class:`JEVTrainer`.

    ``EvalPrediction.predictions`` is the ``[N, Kmax]`` canonical probability
    matrix; ``label_ids`` is ``[N, 3] = (target_idx, qtype_id, num_options)``.
    Output keys are flat (``accuracy``, ``nll``, ``brier``, ``ece``, ``choice_accuracy``,
    ``noul_auroc``, ``score_mae`` ...) so the Trainer prefixes them with ``eval_``.
    """
    max_options = int(getattr(label_space, "max_options", 0) or 0) if label_space is not None else 0

    def _compute(ep: EvalPrediction) -> dict[str, float]:
        preds = ep.predictions
        if isinstance(preds, (tuple, list)):
            preds = preds[0]
        probs = np.asarray(preds, dtype=np.float64)
        labels = np.asarray(ep.label_ids)
        if labels.ndim != 2 or labels.shape[1] < 3:
            raise ValueError(f"Expected label_ids of shape [N, 3], got {labels.shape}")
        if probs.ndim == 1:
            probs = probs[:, None]
        if max_options and probs.shape[1] < max_options:
            probs = np.pad(probs, ((0, 0), (0, max_options - probs.shape[1])))
        summary = summarize(probs, labels[:, 0], labels[:, 1], labels[:, 2])
        flat = flatten_summary(summary)
        return {k: v for k, v in flat.items() if not k.startswith("ds_")}

    return _compute


__all__ = [
    "JEVTrainer",
    "MODEL_INPUT_KEYS",
    "JEV_EXTRA_KEYS",
    "compute_training_schedule",
    "build_training_arguments",
    "compute_metrics_fn",
]
